/* =====================================================================
   storage.js – סנכרון ענן אוטומטי (Google Apps Script), עם הגנות אבטחה
   ומונע דריסות עיוורות באמצעות גרסאות שרת.
   ===================================================================== */
function sanitizeState(s) {
  const obj = v => !!v && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v);
  if (!obj(s) || s.app !== 'schoolDashboard') throw new Error('קובץ / תמונת מצב לא תקינים');
  const text = v => typeof v === 'string' ? v : '';
  const number = v => typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) ? v : 0;
  const id = v => typeof v === 'string' || typeof v === 'number' ? Util.normId(v) : '';
  const clean = v => {
    if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(clean);
    if (obj(v)) return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(v)
      .filter(([k]) => !['__proto__', 'prototype', 'constructor'].includes(k))
      .map(([k, value]) => [k, k === 'tz' ? id(value) : clean(value)]));
    return v === null || ['string', 'boolean'].includes(typeof v) || (typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v)) ? v : null;
  };
  const result = clean(s);
  const bools = new Set(['isTeacher', 'derived', 'hidden', 'isException']);
  const numbers = new Set(['score', 'weight', 'declaredCount', 'updatedAt']);
  for (const key of ['rosterData', ...Config.DATASET_KEYS]) {
    const entry = s[key];
    if (entry === undefined) { delete result[key]; continue; }
    if (entry === null) { result[key] = null; continue; }
    if (!obj(entry) || !Array.isArray(entry.data)) throw new Error(`מבנה נתונים לא תקין: ${key}`);
    const rows = entry.data.filter(obj).map(row => {
      const rec = {};
      for (const [field, value] of Object.entries(row)) {
        if (['__proto__', 'prototype', 'constructor'].includes(field)) continue;
        if (field === 'tz') rec.tz = id(value);
        else if (bools.has(field)) rec[field] = value === true;
        else if (numbers.has(field)) rec[field] = typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
        else if (field === 'cls' && obj(value)) rec.cls = {
          kind: ['num', 'pass', 'none', 'fail'].includes(value.kind) ? value.kind : 'none',
          ...(value.block === true ? { block: true } : {}),
          ...(value.exempt === true ? { exempt: true } : {}),
          ...(typeof value.num === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.num) ? { num: value.num } : {})
        };
        else if (typeof value === 'string' || (typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value))) rec[field] = field === 'scoreRaw' ? value : String(value);
      }
      return rec;
    }).filter(row => row.tz || (key === 'groupsData' && row.isTeacher));
    result[key] = {
      data: key === 'rosterData' ? Config.normalizeRosterRows(rows).rows : rows,
      fileName: text(entry.fileName), uploadedAt: number(entry.uploadedAt),
      ...(key === 'rosterData' ? { ignoredIds: Array.isArray(entry.ignoredIds) ? entry.ignoredIds.map(id).filter(Boolean) : [] } : {})
    };
  }
  const records = (source, convert) => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(obj(source) ? source : {})
    .filter(([tz, value]) => id(tz) && obj(value)).map(([tz, value]) => [id(tz), convert(value, id(tz))]));
  result.rosterOverrides = records(s.rosterOverrides, (v, tz) => ({
    tz, grade: Util.GRADE_KEYS.includes(v.grade) ? v.grade : '',
    homeroom: text(v.homeroom), track: Config.TRACKS.includes(v.track) ? v.track : '',
    hidden: v.hidden === true, name: text(v.name), note: text(v.note).slice(0, 200), updatedAt: number(v.updatedAt)
  }));
  result.studentMeta = records(s.studentMeta, v => ({
    isException: v.isException === true,
    overrideStatus: Config.OVERRIDE_STATUSES.some(status => status.value === v.overrideStatus) ? v.overrideStatus : '',
    trackOverride: Config.TRACKS.includes(v.trackOverride) ? v.trackOverride : '',
    reason: text(v.reason), note: text(v.note)
  }));
  for (const [key, type] of [['classNicknames', 'string'], ['specialEdClasses', 'boolean'], ['rosterMismatchDismissed', 'string']]) {
    result[key] = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(obj(s[key]) ? s[key] : {})
      .filter(([k, v]) => !['__proto__', 'prototype', 'constructor'].includes(k) && typeof v === type)
      .map(([k, v]) => [key === 'rosterMismatchDismissed' ? id(k) : k, v]).filter(([k]) => k));
  }
  return result;
}

const CloudSync = {
  LS: { url: 'sd.cloud.url', clientId: 'sd.cloud.clientId', pass: 'sd.cloud.passcode', meta: 'sd.cloud.meta' },
  status: { kind: 'none', text: '', updatedAt: 0 },
  _suppress: 0,
  _timer: null,
  _revision: 0,
  /* גרסת הענן שהלשונית הזו עצמה סנכרנה לאחרונה; localStorage משותף לכל הלשוניות ולכן אינו משמש לבדיקת דריסה */
  _tabRev: 0,
  _settingsRevision: 0,
  _ready: false,
  _wiping: false,
  _requestController: null,
  _queue: Promise.resolve(),
  _identity: null,
  _authTimer: null,
  _onboarding: false,

  /* ---------- הגדרות ---------- */
  _get(k) { try { return localStorage.getItem(this.LS[k]) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } },
  _set(k, v) { if (v) localStorage.setItem(this.LS[k], v); else localStorage.removeItem(this.LS[k]); },
  url() { return (this._get('url') || '').trim(); },
  clientId() { return this._get('clientId') || Config.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID; },
  passcode() { return this._get('pass'); },
  identity() {
    if (this._identity && this._identity.exp * 1000 <= Date.now()) this.signOutGoogle();
    return this._identity;
  },
  configured() { return !!(this.url() && this.identity()); },
  saveSettings({ url, clientId, passcode }) {
    const previousUrl = this.url(), previousPass = this.passcode(), previousClientId = this.clientId();
    if (url && new URL(String(url).trim()).protocol !== 'https:') throw new Error('כתובת הענן חייבת להשתמש ב-HTTPS');
    if (url !== undefined) this._set('url', String(url).trim());
    if (clientId !== undefined) this._set('clientId', String(clientId).trim());
    if (passcode !== undefined) this._set('pass', passcode);
    if (this.clientId() !== previousClientId) {
      const wasSignedIn = !!this._identity;
      this.signOutGoogle();
      if (!wasSignedIn) window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('sd:google-ready'));
    }
    if (this.url() !== previousUrl || this.passcode() !== previousPass || this.clientId() !== previousClientId) {
      clearTimeout(this._timer);
      this._revision++;
      this._settingsRevision++;
      this._ready = false;
      this._tabRev = 0;
      this._setMeta({ syncedAt: 0, rev: 0 });
      this.emitStatus({ kind: 'none', text: '', updatedAt: 0 });
    }
  },
  acceptGoogleCredential(token) {
    if (this._wiping) throw new Error('לא ניתן להתחבר בזמן מחיקת הנתונים המקומיים');
    let claims;
    try {
      const parts = token.split('.');
      if (parts.length !== 3) throw new Error('invalid token');
      claims = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(this._unb64(parts[1].replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/'))));
    } catch (e) { throw new Error('לא התקבלו פרטי התחברות תקינים מ-Google'); }
    if (!claims || typeof claims.email !== 'string' || !claims.email || claims.aud !== this.clientId() ||
        !Number.isFinite(Number(claims.exp)) || Number(claims.exp) * 1000 <= Date.now()) {
      throw new Error('פרטי ההתחברות פגי תוקף או אינם תקינים. יש להתחבר מחדש עם Google');
    }
    this._identity = { token, email: claims.email, exp: Number(claims.exp) };
    this._revision++;
    this._settingsRevision++;
    this._ready = false;
    clearTimeout(this._authTimer);
    this._authTimer = setTimeout(() => this.signOutGoogle(), Math.min(2147483647, Math.max(0, claims.exp * 1000 - Date.now())));
    this.emitStatus({ kind: this.status.kind === 'conflict' ? 'conflict' : 'none', text: this.status.kind === 'conflict' ? this.status.text : '' });
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('sd:cloud-auth'));
  },
  signOutGoogle() {
    clearTimeout(this._authTimer);
    clearTimeout(this._timer);
    const googleId = window.google && window.google.accounts && window.google.accounts.id;
    if (googleId && typeof googleId.disableAutoSelect === 'function') googleId.disableAutoSelect();
    if (!this._identity) return;
    this._identity = null;
    this._revision++;
    this._settingsRevision++;
    this._ready = false;
    if (this._requestController) this._requestController.abort();
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('sd:cloud-signedout'));
    if (this.status.kind !== 'conflict') this.emitStatus({ kind: 'error', text: 'נדרשת התחברות מחדש עם Google. הנתונים נשמרו מקומית.', error: '' });
  },
  renderGoogleButton(element) {
    const id = window.google && window.google.accounts && window.google.accounts.id;
    if (!element || !id) return;
    id.initialize({ client_id: this.clientId(), callback: response => {
      try { this.acceptGoogleCredential(response.credential); }
      catch (e) { this.emitStatus({ kind: 'error', text: e.message, error: e.message }); }
    } });
    id.renderButton(element, { theme: 'outline', size: 'large', text: 'signin_with', locale: 'he' });
  },
  meta() {
    let m;
    try { m = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(this.LS.meta)); } catch (e) { console.error('cloud metadata', e); }
    return { syncedAt: Number(m?.syncedAt) || 0, dirty: !!m?.dirty, rev: Number.isSafeInteger(m?.rev) && m.rev >= 0 ? m.rev : 0 };
  },
  _setMeta(m) { localStorage.setItem(this.LS.meta, JSON.stringify({ ...this.meta(), ...m })); },
  hasUnpublished() { return !!this.meta().dirty; },

  markDirty() {
    if (this._suppress) return;
    this._revision++;
    this._setMeta({ dirty: true });
    this.emitStatus();
    this._schedulePush();
  },
  _schedulePush() {
    if (!this._suppress && !this._wiping && this.configured() && this.hasUnpublished() && this.status.kind !== 'conflict') {
      clearTimeout(this._timer);
      this._timer = setTimeout(() => this.pushCloudState({ auto: true }).catch(e => {
        console.error('cloud push', e);
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('sd:cloud-auto-error', { detail: { error: e && e.message } }));
      }), 3000);
    }
  },
  _enqueue(fn) {
    const operation = this._queue.then(() => {
      if (this._wiping) throw new Error('הסנכרון חסום בזמן מחיקת הנתונים המקומיים');
      return fn();
    });
    this._queue = operation.catch(e => console.error('cloud sync', e));
    return operation;
  },
  emitStatus(patch) {
    if (patch) this.status = { ...this.status, ...patch };
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('sd:cloud-status', { detail: this.status }));
  },
  _conflict(remote) {
    clearTimeout(this._timer);
    this.emitStatus({ kind: 'conflict', text: 'נשמרו שינויים ממחשב אחר (או מלשונית אחרת). כדי לא למחוק עבודה של אף אחד, השמירה האוטומטית נעצרה — בחרו כיצד להמשיך:' });
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('sd:cloud-conflict', { detail: remote }));
    return 'kept-local';
  },
  async checkCloudMeta() {
    return this._enqueue(async () => {
      const revision = this._revision;
      try {
        const remote = await this._post({ action: 'meta' });
        if (revision !== this._revision) return;
        if (remote.rev > this._tabRev) this._conflict(remote);
      } catch (e) {
        this.emitStatus({ kind: 'error', text: '☁️ בדיקת הענן נכשלה', error: e.message });
        throw e;
      }
    });
  },
  teamJoinLink() {
    if (!this.url()) throw new Error('יש להגדיר כתובת ענן לפני שיתוף');
    const json = JSON.stringify({ url: this.url(), ...(this.clientId() !== Config.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID ? { clientId: this.clientId() } : {}) });
    const hash = this._b64(new TextEncoder().encode(json)).replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '');
    return `${location.origin}${location.pathname}${location.search}#cloud=${hash}`;
  },

  /* ---------- בניית מצב מלא וטעינתו ---------- */
  buildState() {
    const ds = k => Config.getDataset(k);
    return {
      app: 'schoolDashboard', version: 1, updatedAt: Date.now(),
      rosterData: Config.getRosterEntry(),
      bagrutData: ds('bagrutData'), groupsData: ds('groupsData'), sadinData: ds('sadinData'),
      rosterOverrides: Config.data.rosterOverrides,
      studentMeta: Config.data.traineeExceptions,
      customQuestionnaires: Config.rulesForGrade('12'),
      cohortProfiles: Config.data.cohortProfiles, gradeCohortMap: Config.data.gradeCohortMap,
      classNicknames: Config.data.classNicknames, specialEdClasses: Config.data.specialEdClasses,
      rosterMismatchDismissed: Config.data.rosterMismatchDismissed,
      fileTimestamps: Object.fromEntries(['masterRoster', ...Config.DATASET_KEYS].map(k => {
        const e = k === 'masterRoster' ? Config.getRosterEntry() : ds(k);
        return [k, e ? { uploadedAt: e.uploadedAt || 0, fileName: e.fileName || '' } : null];
      }))
    };
  },

  async applyState(s, { userEdit = false } = {}) {
    if (this._wiping) throw new Error('לא ניתן לטעון נתונים בזמן מחיקת ההפעלה המקומית');
    if (userEdit) { const denied = Config.requireEdit(); if (denied) throw new Error(denied.error); }
    s = sanitizeState(s);
    if (s.customQuestionnaires) {
      const err = Config.validateRules(s.customQuestionnaires);
      if (err) throw new Error(`כללי הזכאות בתמונת המצב לא תקינים: ${err}`);
    }
    if (s.cohortProfiles) {
      const list = Array.isArray(s.cohortProfiles) ? s.cohortProfiles : Object.values(s.cohortProfiles || {});
      const bad = list.find(p => p && p.rules && Config.validateRules(p.rules));
      if (bad) throw new Error(`כללי המחזור "${bad.name || bad.id}" בתמונת המצב לא תקינים: ${Config.validateRules(bad.rules)}`);
    }
    const revision = this._revision;
    const written = [];
    let committed = false;
    try {
      for (const key of Config.DATASET_KEYS) {
        if (!(key in s)) continue;
        written.push(key);
        if (s[key] === null) await Config._storeDelete(key, userEdit);
        else {
          const r = await Config._storePut(key, s[key], userEdit);
          if (!r.ok) throw new Error(r.error);
        }
        if (revision !== this._revision) return 'kept-local';
      }
      // Suppress only our synchronous commit, never user edits during IndexedDB awaits.
      if (userEdit) { const denied = Config.requireEdit(); if (denied) throw new Error(denied.error); }
      const previous = Config.data, activeCohort = Config._activeCohort;
      const savedKeys = [Config.KEYS.config, Config.KEYS.masterRoster, Config.KEYS.rosterCleared];
      const previousStorage = savedKeys.map(key => localStorage.getItem(key));
      const previousRoster = Config._cache.masterRoster;
      this._suppress++;
      try {
        Config.data = Util.clone(previous);
        const d = Config.data;
        d.rosterOverrides = s.rosterOverrides;
        d.traineeExceptions = s.studentMeta;
        d.classNicknames = s.classNicknames;
        d.specialEdClasses = s.specialEdClasses;
        d.rosterMismatchDismissed = s.rosterMismatchDismissed;
        Config.applyCohortState(s.cohortProfiles, s.gradeCohortMap, s.customQuestionnaires);
        Config._syncQuestionnaires();
        const saved = Config._write(Config.KEYS.config, Config.data);
        if (!saved.ok) throw new Error(saved.error);
        Config.emit('config-changed');
        if (s.rosterData !== undefined) {
          if (s.rosterData === null) {
            localStorage.removeItem(Config.KEYS.masterRoster);
            localStorage.setItem(Config.KEYS.rosterCleared, '1');
          } else {
            const w = Config._write(Config.KEYS.masterRoster, s.rosterData);
            if (!w.ok) throw new Error(w.error);
            localStorage.removeItem(Config.KEYS.rosterCleared);
          }
          Config._cache.masterRoster = s.rosterData;
        }
        for (const key of written) Config._cache[key] = s[key];
        Config._cache = { ...Config._cache };
        Config._invalidateRoster();
        committed = true;
        return 'applied';
      } catch (e) {
        Config.data = previous;
        Config._activeCohort = activeCohort;
        Config._cache.masterRoster = previousRoster;
        savedKeys.forEach((key, i) => {
          if (previousStorage[i] === null) localStorage.removeItem(key);
          else localStorage.setItem(key, previousStorage[i]);
        });
        Config._invalidateRoster();
        throw e;
      } finally { this._suppress--; }
    } finally {
      if (!committed && !this._wiping) {
        // The cache still holds the latest local edits, including edits made while awaiting.
        for (const key of written) {
          let current;
          do {
            current = Config.getDataset(key);
            if (current) {
              const r = await Config._storePut(key, current);
              if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`שחזור הנתונים המקומיים נכשל: ${r.error}`);
            } else await Config._storeDelete(key);
          } while (current !== Config.getDataset(key));
        }
      }
    }
  },

  /* ---------- הצפנה (קוד בית-ספר) - מבוססת AES-GCM בלבד ---------- */
  _b64(buf) {
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(buf), chunks = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 32768) chunks.push(String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + 32768)));
    return btoa(chunks.join(''));
  },
  _unb64(str) {
    const binary = atob(str), bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
    for (let start = 0; start < binary.length; start += 32768) {
      const end = Math.min(start + 32768, binary.length);
      for (let i = start; i < end; i++) bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
    }
    return bytes;
  },
  async _key(pass, salt, iterations = 600000) {
    const base = await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', new TextEncoder().encode(pass), 'PBKDF2', false, ['deriveKey']);
    return crypto.subtle.deriveKey({ name: 'PBKDF2', salt, iterations, hash: 'SHA-256' }, base, { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 }, false, ['encrypt', 'decrypt']);
  },
  async seal(obj, { requirePasscode = true } = {}) {
    const text = JSON.stringify(obj), pass = this.passcode();
    if (!pass) {
      if (requirePasscode) throw new Error('יש להגדיר קוד בית-ספר להצפנה לפני העלאה לענן');
      return { enc: 'none', data: text };
    }
    if (!(window.crypto && crypto.subtle)) throw new Error('הדפדפן אינו תומך בהצפנה מאובטחת');
    const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(text);
    const salt = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16)), iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));
    const ct = await crypto.subtle.encrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv }, await this._key(pass, salt), bytes);
    return { enc: 'aes2', salt: this._b64(salt), iv: this._b64(iv), data: this._b64(ct) };
  },
  async open(env) {
    if (!env || typeof env !== 'object') throw new Error('תמונת מצב ריקה בענן');
    if (env.enc === 'none') {
      throw new Error('הנתונים בענן אינם מוצפנים. יש להשתמש בתמונת מצב מוצפנת ולהזין קוד בית-ספר');
    }
    const pass = this.passcode();
    if (!pass) throw new Error('הנתונים בענן מוצפנים – יש להזין קוד בית-ספר בהגדרות הסנכרון');
    try {
      if (env.enc === 'aes1' || env.enc === 'aes2') {
        if (!(window.crypto && crypto.subtle)) throw new Error('הדפדפן אינו תומך בפענוח');
        const pt = await crypto.subtle.decrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv: this._unb64(env.iv) }, await this._key(pass, this._unb64(env.salt), env.enc === 'aes1' ? 100000 : 600000), this._unb64(env.data));
        return JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(pt));
      }
    } catch (e) { throw new Error('קוד בית-ספר שגוי או נתונים פגומים'); }
    throw new Error('פורמט הצפנה לא מוכר או שאינו נתמך מסיבות אבטחה');
  },

  /** שגיאת רשת / ביטול בקשה → הודעה ברורה בעברית; שגיאות אחרות (כבר בעברית או לא קשורות לרשת) עוברות כמות שהן */
  _networkError(e) {
    const text = String((e && e.message) || '');
    if (e && e.name === 'AbortError') return new Error('החיבור לענן פג (זמן תגובה ארוך מדי). הנתונים נשמרו מקומית.');
    if (navigator.onLine === false || (e && e.name === 'TypeError') || /Failed to fetch|Load failed|NetworkError|Network request failed/i.test(text)) {
      return new Error('אין חיבור לרשת או ששרת הענן אינו זמין. הנתונים נשמרו מקומית; אפשר לנסות לסנכרן שוב.');
    }
    return e;
  },

  /* ---------- תקשורת ---------- */
  async _post(body) {
    if (this._wiping) throw new Error('הסנכרון חסום בזמן מחיקת הנתונים המקומיים');
    const url = this.url();
    if (!url) throw new Error('לא הוגדרה כתובת סנכרון ענן');
    if (new URL(url).protocol !== 'https:') throw new Error('כתובת הענן חייבת להשתמש ב-HTTPS');
    const identity = this.identity();
    if (!identity) throw new Error('נדרשת התחברות מחדש עם Google. הנתונים נשמרו מקומית.');
    const controller = new AbortController();
    this._requestController = controller;
    try {
      let res;
      try {
        res = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' }, body: JSON.stringify({ ...body, idToken: identity.token }), signal: controller.signal });
      } catch (e) { throw this._networkError(e); }
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`שגיאת שרת ${res.status}`);
      const json = await res.json();
      if (!json.ok && !json.conflict) {
        if (json.error === 'unauthorized' || json.error === 'token_expired') {
          if (this._identity === identity) this.signOutGoogle();
          throw new Error('ההתחברות פגה או נדחתה. יש להתחבר מחדש עם Google; הנתונים נשמרו מקומית.');
        }
        const messages = {
          forbidden: 'חשבון Google אינו מורשה לגשת לנתוני בית הספר',
          auth_unavailable: 'לא ניתן לאמת את חשבון Google כרגע',
          file_id_missing: 'לא הוגדר קובץ ענן. יש לפנות למנהל המערכת',
          multiple_files: 'נמצאו כמה קובצי ענן. יש לפנות למנהל המערכת',
          invalid_envelope: 'מעטפת ההצפנה אינה תקינה',
          internal_error: 'שגיאה פנימית בשרת הענן'
        };
        throw new Error(messages[json.error] || 'שרת הענן דחה את הבקשה');
      }
      return json;
    } finally {
      if (this._requestController === controller) this._requestController = null;
    }
  },

  pushCloudState(opts = {}) {
    return this._enqueue(async () => {
      await Config.ready;
      if (opts.auto && (!this.configured() || !this.hasUnpublished())) return 'current';
      if (!opts.force && this.status.kind === 'conflict') return 'kept-local';
      if (!this._ready && !opts.force) {
        const result = await this._pullCloudState();
        if (result === 'kept-local') return result;
        this._ready = true;
      }
      return this._pushCloudState(opts);
    });
  },

  async _pushCloudState(opts = {}) {
    clearTimeout(this._timer);
    const revision = this._revision;
    const settingsRevision = this._settingsRevision;
    const state = this.buildState();
    this.emitStatus({ kind: 'busy', text: '☁️ מסנכרן...' });
    try {
      const payload = await this.seal(state);
      const bodyData = {
        action: 'push',
        baseRev: this._tabRev,
        force: opts.force === true,
        payload
      };
      if (revision !== this._revision) { this._schedulePush(); return 'kept-local'; }
      const r = await this._post(bodyData);
      if (settingsRevision !== this._settingsRevision) return 'kept-local';
      if (r.conflict) return this._conflict(r);
      this._ready = true;
      this._tabRev = r.rev;
      this._setMeta({ dirty: revision !== this._revision, syncedAt: r.updatedAt, rev: r.rev });
      this.emitStatus({ kind: 'ok', updatedAt: r.updatedAt, text: '' });
      this._schedulePush();
      return r;
    } catch (e) {
      this.emitStatus({ kind: 'error', text: '☁️ הסנכרון נכשל', error: e.message });
      throw e;
    }
  },

  pullCloudState(opts = {}) {
    return this._enqueue(async () => {
      await Config.ready;
      const result = await this._pullCloudState(opts);
      this._ready = result !== 'kept-local';
      if (result !== 'kept-local') this._schedulePush();
      return result;
    });
  },
  async _pullCloudState(opts = {}) {
    const revision = this._revision;
    this.emitStatus({ kind: 'busy', text: '☁️ מסנכרן...' });
    try {
      if (!this.url()) throw new Error('לא הוגדרה כתובת סנכרון ענן');
      const json = await this._post({ action: 'pull' });
      if (revision !== this._revision) return this._conflict(json);
      if (!json.payload) {
        this._tabRev = json.rev;
        this._setMeta({ rev: json.rev, syncedAt: json.updatedAt });
        this.emitStatus({ kind: 'none', text: '☁️ אין עדיין נתונים בענן' }); return 'empty';
      }
      if (json.payload.enc === 'none') throw new Error('הנתונים בענן אינם מוצפנים. יש להשתמש בתמונת מצב מוצפנת ולהזין קוד בית-ספר');
      const meta = this.meta();
      if (!opts.force && json.rev === this._tabRev && meta.syncedAt) {
        this.emitStatus({ kind: 'ok', updatedAt: meta.syncedAt, text: '' });
        return 'current';
      }
      const state = await this.open(json.payload);
      if (this._revision !== revision || (!opts.force && this.hasUnpublished())) {
        return this._conflict(json);
      }
      if (await this.applyState(state) === 'kept-local' || revision !== this._revision) return this._conflict(json);
      this._tabRev = json.rev;
      this._setMeta({ dirty: false, syncedAt: json.updatedAt, rev: json.rev });
      this.emitStatus({ kind: 'ok', updatedAt: json.updatedAt, text: '' });
      return 'applied';
    } catch (e) {
      this.emitStatus({ kind: 'error', text: '☁️ הסנכרון נכשל', error: e.message });
      throw e;
    }
  },

  /* ---------- גיבוי לקובץ ---------- */
  async exportBackup() {
    await Config.ready;
    if (this._wiping) throw new Error('לא ניתן לייצא בזמן מחיקת ההפעלה המקומית');
    const backupObj = { ...this.buildState(), mappingProfiles: Config.data.mappingProfiles };
    const backup = this.passcode() ? await this.seal(backupObj) : backupObj;
    if (this._wiping) throw new Error('הייצוא בוטל בזמן מחיקת ההפעלה המקומית');
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(backup)], { type: 'application/json' });
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.download = `school-dashboard-backup-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.json`;
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 1000);
  },
  importBackup(text) { return this._enqueue(() => this._importBackup(text)); },
  async _importBackup(text) {
    await Config.ready;
    let s;
    try { s = JSON.parse(text); } catch (e) { throw new Error('קובץ JSON לא תקין'); }
    if (s && (s.enc === 'aes2' || s.enc === 'aes1')) {
      if (!this.passcode()) throw new Error("קובץ הגיבוי מוצפן — יש להזין את קוד בית-הספר למעלה וללחוץ על 'שמירת הגדרות' לפני השחזור.");
      s = await this.open(s);
    }
    const denied = Config.requireEdit(); if (denied) throw new Error(denied.error);
    if (this._wiping) throw new Error('השחזור בוטל בזמן מחיקת ההפעלה המקומית');
    const LEGACY_KEYS = ['masterRoster', 'bagrutRules', 'cohortProfiles', 'classNicknames', 'specialEdClasses', 'rosterOverrides', 'traineeExceptions', 'mappingProfiles'];
    const isFull = s && typeof s === 'object' && !Array.isArray(s) && ('fileTimestamps' in s || 'studentMeta' in s || 'rosterData' in s);
    if (s && typeof s === 'object' && !Array.isArray(s) && !isFull && LEGACY_KEYS.some(k => k in s)) {
      await Config.importJSON(JSON.stringify(s), { silent: true });
      this.markDirty();
      return 'legacy';
    }
    if (await this.applyState(s, { userEdit: true }) === 'kept-local') throw new Error('בוצעו שינויים בזמן השחזור. הנתונים המקומיים נשמרו; יש לנסות שוב.');
    this.markDirty();
    if (s.mappingProfiles && typeof s.mappingProfiles === 'object' && !Array.isArray(s.mappingProfiles)) {
      const denied = Config.requireEdit(); if (denied) throw new Error(denied.error);
      Config.data.mappingProfiles = s.mappingProfiles;
      const saved = Config.save();
      if (!saved.ok) throw new Error(saved.error);
    }
    return 'full';
  },

  async wipeLocalSession() {
    this._suppress++;
    clearTimeout(this._timer);
    this._wiping = true;
    Config._sessionClosing = true;
    this.signOutGoogle();
    this._revision++;
    this._settingsRevision++;
    this._tabRev = 0;
    this._requestController?.abort();
    // Drain in-flight/queued work before deletion so late responses cannot restore data.
    await this._queue;
    await Config.ready;
    const dashboardKeys = new Set([...Object.keys(Config.KEYS), ...Object.values(Config.KEYS)]);
    for (let i = localStorage.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const key = localStorage.key(i);
      if (key && (key.startsWith('sd.') || dashboardKeys.has(key))) localStorage.removeItem(key);
    }
    localStorage.removeItem('apiToken');
    if (Config._idb) { Config._idb.close(); Config._idb = null; }
    if (typeof indexedDB !== 'undefined') {
      await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const request = indexedDB.deleteDatabase('schoolDashboard');
        request.onsuccess = () => resolve();
        request.onerror = () => reject(new Error('מחיקת מסד הנתונים המקומי נכשלה'));
        request.onblocked = () => reject(new Error('מחיקת מסד הנתונים נחסמה; יש לסגור לשוניות אחרות ולנסות שוב'));
      });
    }
    Config._cache = {};
    Config.data = Config.defaults();
    Config._ignoredCache = null;
    Config._invalidateRoster();
    location.reload();
  },

  init() {
    localStorage.removeItem('sd.cloud.token');
    localStorage.removeItem('sd.cloud.apiToken');
    localStorage.removeItem('apiToken');
    this._tabRev = this.meta().rev || 0;
    if (location.hash.startsWith('#cloud=')) {
      const hash = location.hash.slice(7);
      history.replaceState(null, '', location.pathname + location.search);
      try {
        const settings = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(this._unb64(hash.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/'))));
        if (!settings || typeof settings.url !== 'string' || !settings.url ||
            (settings.clientId !== undefined && typeof settings.clientId !== 'string')) throw new Error('פרטי הצטרפות חסרים');
        this.saveSettings({ url: settings.url, clientId: settings.clientId || '' });
        this._onboarding = true;
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('cloud onboarding', e);
        this.emitStatus({ kind: 'error', text: 'קישור ההצטרפות לענן אינו תקין', error: e.message });
      }
    }
    const googleScript = document.getElementById('googleIdentityScript');
    if (googleScript) googleScript.addEventListener('load', () => window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('sd:google-ready')));
    ['config-changed', 'dataset-changed', 'roster-changed', 'rules-changed', 'exceptions-changed'].forEach(n => window.addEventListener(`sd:${n}`, e => {
      if (n !== 'config-changed' || !e.detail || !e.detail.localOnly) this.markDirty();
    }));
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible' && this.configured()) this.checkCloudMeta().catch(e => console.error('cloud meta', e));
    });
    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', e => {
      if (!this._wiping && this.hasUnpublished()) { e.preventDefault(); e.returnValue = ''; }
    });
  }
};
CloudSync.init();

(function () {
  'use strict';
  const esc = value => Util.esc(value);
  const badge = (text, className = '') => `<span class="badge ${esc(className)}">${esc(text)}</span>`;
  const signalBadge = (signalOrId, customText) => {
    const id = typeof signalOrId === 'string' ? signalOrId : signalOrId.id;
    const entry = window.SignalEngine.CATALOG[id];
    if (!entry) throw new Error('Unknown signal: ' + id);
    return badge(customText == null ? entry.label : customText, entry.badgeClass);
  };
  const overallBadge = overall => {
    const id = {
      ok: 'bagrut.eligible', watch: 'bagrut.watch', risk: 'bagrut.risk',
      unknown: 'bagrut.unknown', twelve: 'bagrut.twelve'
    }[overall];
    if (!id) throw new Error('Unknown overall status: ' + overall);
    return signalBadge(id);
  };

  const buildBagrutBreakdown = (t, ev, opts = {}) => {
    const isTrack12 = ev.tier === 5;
    const noData = !isTrack12 && (opts.noData || ev.tier === 0);
    const enforceUnits = !isTrack12 && !noData && ev.rank >= 12 && ev.mode === 'full' && !ev.adaptive;
    const totalUnits = Number.isFinite(ev.totalUnits) && !noData ? ev.totalUnits : null;
    const subjects = [], internals = [], other = [];
    const active = isTrack12 || noData ? [] : ev.blockers.filter(b => !b.info);
    const junior = ev.rank < 12 && !ev.manual;
    const addSubject = (name, tags) => {
      if (!tags.length) return;
      const existing = subjects.find(s => s.name === name);
      const item = existing || { name, tags: [] };
      tags.forEach(tag => { if (!item.tags.some(x => x.label === tag.label)) item.tags.push(tag); });
      if (!existing) subjects.push(item);
    };
    const tag = (code, text) => ({ code: code ? String(code) : '', label: code ? `${code} · ${text}` : text });
    if (!isTrack12 && !noData) {
      ev.subjects.forEach(subject => {
        const tags = [];
        if (junior) {
          ev.paceInfo.failedList.filter(x => x.subject === subject.name).forEach(x => tags.push(tag(x.code, 'נכשל')));
          ev.paceInfo.missingDueList.filter(x => x.subject === subject.name).forEach(x => tags.push(tag(x.code, 'חסר')));
          subject.comps.filter(c => c.state === 'exempt' && ev.paceInfo.blockedSubjects.includes(subject.name))
            .forEach(c => tags.push(tag(c.code, 'החלטה')));
        } else {
          const blockers = active.filter(b => b.gate === 1 && b.k !== 'units' &&
            (b.subject === subject.id || b.text.includes(subject.name)));
          blockers.forEach(b => {
            const match = b.text.match(/שאלון\s+([\d/]+)/);
            if (match) {
              const comp = subject.comps.find(c => String(c.code) === match[1]);
              tags.push(tag(match[1], b.k === 'bagrut-block' ? 'חסם' : comp && comp.failed ? 'נכשל' : 'חסר'));
            }
            else if (['fail', 'severe', 'critical', 'comp-over'].includes(b.k)) {
              const failed = ev.paceInfo.failedList.filter(x => x.subject === subject.name);
              if (failed.length) failed.forEach(x => tags.push(tag(x.code, 'נכשל')));
              else tags.push(tag('', b.k === 'comp-over' ? 'מעבר למכסת הקיזוז' : 'נדרש מעבר'));
            } else if (b.k === 'exempt-review') tags.push(tag('', 'נדרשת החלטה'));
            else if (b.k === 'enroll') tags.push(tag('', 'חסר שיבוץ'));
            else if (b.k === 'reverse30' || b.k === 'reverse-pending') tags.push(tag('', 'נדרש תיקון ציון'));
            else tags.push(tag('', 'חסר ציון'));
          });
        }
        addSubject(subject.name, tags);
      });
      active.filter(b => b.gate === 2).forEach(b => {
        const candidate = ev.major.cands.find(c => b.text.includes(c.name));
        const core = candidate && candidate.coreId && ev.subjects.find(s => s.id === candidate.coreId);
        const name = core ? core.name : candidate ? `מגמה: ${candidate.name}` : 'מגמה';
        const match = b.text.match(/שאלון\s+([\d/]+)/);
        const label = b.k === 'major-none' ? b.text :
          b.k === 'exempt-review' ? 'נדרשת החלטה' : b.k === 'major-fail' ? 'נדרש מעבר' :
          b.k === 'bagrut-block' ? 'חסם' : candidate && candidate.failed ? 'חסר או נכשל' : 'חסר';
        addSubject(name, match ? match[1].split('/').map(code => tag(code, label)) : [tag('', label)]);
      });
      active.filter(b => b.gate === 3).forEach(b => {
        const item = ev.internals.find(i => b.text.includes(i.name));
        const name = item ? item.name : b.text;
        if (!internals.some(i => i.name === name)) internals.push({ name, text: b.text });
      });
      if (ev.manual) other.push('הכרעה ידנית; הפירוט מציג את החישוב האוטומטי');
      if (ev.manualReview && ev.manualReview.required) other.push('נדרשת החלטת רכזת');
      active.filter(b => b.k !== 'units' && b.gate !== 2 && b.gate !== 3 &&
        !ev.subjects.some(s => b.subject === s.id || b.text.includes(s.name)))
        .forEach(b => { if (!other.includes(b.text)) other.push(b.text); });
    }
    const subjectCount = subjects.length, internalCount = internals.length;
    const questionnaireCount = subjects.reduce((n, s) => n + new Set(s.tags.filter(x => x.code).map(x => x.code)).size, 0);
    const unitsText = totalUnits == null ? '—' : enforceUnits ? `${totalUnits}/21 יח״ל` : `${totalUnits} יח״ל נצברו`;
    const subjectSummary = s => {
      const count = new Set(s.tags.filter(x => x.code).map(x => x.code)).size;
      return count ? `${s.name} (${count} ${count === 1 ? 'שאלון' : 'שאלונים'})` : `${s.name} · ${s.tags[0].label}`;
    };
    const cellSummary = isTrack12 ? 'מסלול 12 שנות לימוד' : noData ? 'לא ניתן לקבוע ללא נתוני בגרות' :
      subjectCount >= 3 ? `${subjectCount} מקצועות · ${internalCount} פנימיים · ${unitsText}` :
      [...subjects.map(subjectSummary), internalCount ? `${internalCount} פנימיים` : '',
        enforceUnits && totalUnits < 21 ? unitsText : '', ...other].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') || 'לא אותרו חסמים';
    const tooltipLines = isTrack12 || noData ? [cellSummary] : [
      ...subjects.map(s => `${s.name}: ${s.tags.map(x => x.label).join(', ')}`),
      ...(internals.length ? [`פנימיים: ${internals.map(i => i.name).join(', ')}`] : []),
      ...(enforceUnits && totalUnits < 21 ? [unitsText] : []), ...other
    ].slice(0, 3);
    if (!tooltipLines.length) tooltipLines.push('לא אותרו חסמים');
    return {
      tz: t.tz, name: t.name, className: opts.className || t.classKey,
      status: isTrack12 ? 'מסלול 12 שנות לימוד' : noData ? 'לא ניתן לקבוע' :
        ev.manualReview && ev.manualReview.required ? 'נדרשת החלטת רכזת' :
        junior ? ev.paceText : ev.tierText,
      isTrack12, noData, enforceUnits, totalUnits, subjects, internals, other,
      subjectCount, internalCount, questionnaireCount, cellSummary, tooltipLines
    };
  };

  const renderBagrutBreakdown = (b, opts = {}) => {
    const neutral = b.isTrack12 || b.noData;
    const units = b.totalUnits == null ? '—' : b.enforceUnits ? `${b.totalUnits}/21` : String(b.totalUnits);
    const unitLabel = b.enforceUnits ? 'יח״ל לזכאות' : 'יח״ל שנצברו עד כה';
    const metric = (label, value, warning) => `<div class="bg-bd-metric${warning && !neutral ? ' bg-bd-metric-warn' : ''}"><span>${esc(label)}</span><strong>${esc(value)}</strong></div>`;
    const header = opts.showHeader ? `<header class="bg-bd-header"><div><h2>${esc(b.name)}</h2><p>${esc(b.className)} · ${esc(b.status)}</p></div>
      ${opts.showPin ? `<button type="button" class="bg-bd-pin" data-act="bagrutPin" data-tz="${esc(b.tz)}" aria-pressed="${!!opts.pinned}" aria-label="${opts.pinned ? 'הסרה ממעקב צמוד' : 'הוספה למעקב צמוד'}">${opts.pinned ? '★' : '☆'}</button>` : ''}</header>` : '';
    const subjectRows = b.subjects.map(s => `<li><strong>${esc(s.name)}</strong><span class="bg-bd-tags">${s.tags.map(x => `<span class="bg-bd-tag">${esc(x.label)}</span>`).join('')}</span></li>`).join('');
    const internalTags = b.internals.map(i => `<span class="bg-bd-tag" title="${esc(i.text)}">${esc(i.name)}</span>`).join('');
    const metrics = `<div class="bg-bd-metrics">
      ${metric(unitLabel, units, b.enforceUnits && b.totalUnits < 21)}
      ${metric('מקצועות חסרים או חסומים', neutral ? '—' : b.subjectCount, b.subjectCount > 0)}
      ${metric('חסמים פנימיים', neutral ? '—' : b.internalCount, b.internalCount > 0)}</div>`;
    const open360 = opts.showOpen360 ? `<button type="button" class="bg-bd-open" data-act="openProfile" data-tz="${esc(b.tz)}">פתח כרטיס 360 מלא</button>` : '';
    if (opts.matrixSubjects) {
      const cellBadge = (text, state, forecast = false) => `<span class="bagrut-cell bagrut-cell-${esc(state)}${state === 'bad' ? ' score-alert' : ''}${forecast ? ' bagrut-cell-forecast' : ''}">${esc(text)}</span>`;
      const rows = opts.matrixSubjects.map(s => `<li class="bg-drawer-subject${s.state === 'bad' && !s.forecastOnly ? ' bg-drawer-subject-blocked' : ''}"><span class="bg-drawer-subject-name">${esc(s.label)}</span>${cellBadge(s.text, s.state, s.forecastOnly)}${s.more ? `<span class="bagrut-more">+${esc(s.more)}</span>` : ''}${s.gap ? `<span class="bg-drawer-subject-gap">${esc(s.gap)}</span>` : ''}</li>`).join('');
      const internalContent = neutral ? (b.isTrack12 ? 'מסלול 12 שנות לימוד' : 'לא ניתן לקבוע ללא נתוני בגרות') : internalTags || '<span>לא אותרו חסמים פנימיים</span>';
      return `<div class="bg-bd"><div class="bg-drawer-body">${header}${metrics}
        <section class="bg-bd-section"><h3>מקצועות</h3><ul class="bg-drawer-subjects">${rows}</ul></section>
        <section class="bg-bd-section"><h3>חסמים במקצועות פנימיים</h3><div class="bg-bd-tags">${internalContent}</div></section>
        ${subjectRows ? `<details class="bg-drawer-exams"><summary>פירוט שאלונים לטיפול</summary><ul class="bg-bd-subjects">${subjectRows}</ul></details>` : ''}
        ${b.other.length ? `<section class="bg-bd-section"><h3>נוסף לבדיקה</h3><ul>${b.other.map(text => `<li>${esc(text)}</li>`).join('')}</ul></section>` : ''}</div>${open360}</div>`;
    }
    return `<div class="bg-bd">${header}${metrics}
      ${neutral ? `<p class="bg-bd-note">${esc(b.cellSummary)}</p>` : `<section class="bg-bd-section"><h3>בחינות ומקצועות לטיפול</h3><ul class="bg-bd-subjects">${subjectRows || '<li>לא אותרו חסמים במקצועות</li>'}</ul></section>
      <section class="bg-bd-section"><h3>חסמים במקצועות פנימיים</h3><div class="bg-bd-tags">${internalTags || '<span>לא אותרו חסמים פנימיים</span>'}</div></section>
      ${b.other.length ? `<section class="bg-bd-section"><h3>נוסף לבדיקה</h3><ul>${b.other.map(text => `<li>${esc(text)}</li>`).join('')}</ul></section>` : ''}`}
      ${open360}</div>`;
  };

  window.UI = Object.freeze({ esc, badge, signalBadge, overallBadge, buildBagrutBreakdown, renderBagrutBreakdown });
})();


========================================
FILE: js\views\bagrutView.js

(function () {
  'use strict';
  const E = Util.esc;
  const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
  const setHtml = (id, html) => { const el = $(id); if (el) el.innerHTML = html; return el; };
  const natural = (a, b) => String(a).localeCompare(String(b), 'he', { numeric: true });
  const isUnknown = signal => signal.pedagogical.some(f => f.id === 'bagrut.unknown');
  const noBagrutData = (signal, sim) =>
    !signal.prereqs.hasBagrut && !(sim.useCurrent && signal.prereqs.hasSadin);

  const finalOptions = { fullMode: true, useCurrent: false, ignoreInternals: false, plus5: false };

  const PINS_KEY = 'schoolDashboard.bagrut.pins.v1';
  const DEFERRED_KEY = 'sd.bagrut.deferred.v1';
  const WIDTHS_KEY = 'schoolDashboard.bagrut.colWidths.v1';
  const WORK_COLUMNS = {
    list: [['name', 'חניך', 150], ['class', 'כיתה', 85], ['issues', 'מה חוסם או דורש בדיקה', 240], ['order', 'סדר ברשימה', 135]],
    subjects: [['subject', 'מקצוע', 140], ['exams', 'תוצאות שאלונים', 180], ['status', 'סטטוס נוכחי', 135], ['target', 'ציון נדרש ב-30%', 165], ['details', 'פרטי חישוב', 110]]
  };
  let pinsCache = null;
  let deferredCache = null;
  let widthsCache = null;
  function loadWorkWidths() {
    if (widthsCache) return widthsCache;
    widthsCache = { list: {}, subjects: {} };
    try {
      const saved = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem(WIDTHS_KEY) || '{}');
      if (!saved || typeof saved !== 'object' || Array.isArray(saved)) throw new Error('רוחבי העמודות אינם תקינים');
      Object.keys(WORK_COLUMNS).forEach(kind => {
        const group = saved[kind];
        if (group == null) return;
        if (typeof group !== 'object' || Array.isArray(group)) throw new Error(`רוחבי עמודות ${kind} אינם תקינים`);
        WORK_COLUMNS[kind].forEach(([key]) => {
          if (group[key] == null) return;
          if (!Number.isFinite(group[key]) || group[key] <= 0) throw new Error(`רוחב עמודה ${key} אינו תקין`);
          widthsCache[kind][key] = group[key];
        });
      });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('טעינת רוחבי עמודות הזכאות נכשלה', e);
      widthsCache = { list: {}, subjects: {} };
    }
    return widthsCache;
  }
  function saveWorkWidths() {
    try { window.localStorage.setItem(WIDTHS_KEY, JSON.stringify(loadWorkWidths())); }
    catch (e) { console.error('שמירת רוחבי עמודות הזכאות נכשלה', e); }
  }
  function workColumns(kind) {
    const saved = loadWorkWidths()[kind];
    const customized = Object.keys(saved).length > 0;
    const columns = WORK_COLUMNS[kind].map(([key, label, defaultWidth]) => ({ key, label, width: saved[key] || defaultWidth }));
    const width = columns.reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.width, 0);
    return {
      width: customized ? `${width}px` : '100%',
      cols: columns.map(c => `<col style="width:${customized ? `${c.width}px` : `${c.width / width * 100}%`}">`).join(''),
      headers: columns.map(c => `<th scope="col" data-ck="${c.key}"><span class="bagrut-col-label">${E(c.label)}</span><span class="col-resizer" aria-hidden="true" title="גרור לשינוי רוחב עמודה (לחיצה כפולה לאיפוס רוחבי הטבלה)"></span></th>`).join('')
    };
  }
  function lockWorkColWidths(table) {
    const kind = table.dataset.widthGroup, widths = loadWorkWidths()[kind];
    const cols = table.querySelectorAll('colgroup col');
    const headers = table.querySelectorAll('thead th');
    headers.forEach((th, index) => {
      const width = th.getBoundingClientRect().width;
      cols[index].style.width = `${width}px`;
      widths[th.dataset.ck] = width;
    });
    table.style.width = `${[...cols].reduce((sum, col) => sum + parseFloat(col.style.width), 0)}px`;
  }
  function setWorkColWidth(table, index, key, width) {
    const kind = table.dataset.widthGroup, widths = loadWorkWidths();
    if (!WORK_COLUMNS[kind] || !WORK_COLUMNS[kind].some(c => c[0] === key)) throw new Error('עמודת זכאות לא מוכרת');
    const cols = table.querySelectorAll('colgroup col');
    const otherWidth = [...cols].reduce((sum, col, i) => sum + (i === index ? 0 : parseFloat(col.style.width)), 0);
    const container = table.parentElement, style = getComputedStyle(container);
    const containerWidth = container.clientWidth - parseFloat(style.paddingLeft) - parseFloat(style.paddingRight);
    // Shrinking below the container width would redistribute space to the other columns.
    const w = Math.max(56, containerWidth - otherWidth, width);
    if (cols[index]) cols[index].style.width = `${w}px`;
    table.style.width = `${[...cols].reduce((sum, c) => sum + parseFloat(c.style.width), 0)}px`;
    widths[kind][key] = w;
  }
  const resizeHandle = e => e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#bagrut-table table[data-width-group] .col-resizer');
  document.addEventListener('mousedown', e => {
    const h = resizeHandle(e);
    if (!h || e.button !== 0) return;
    e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault();
    const th = h.closest('th'), table = th.closest('table'), key = th.dataset.ck, index = th.cellIndex;
    const startX = e.clientX, startW = th.getBoundingClientRect().width;
    let dragged = false;
    document.body.classList.add('col-resizing');
    const move = ev => {
      if (!dragged && ev.clientX === startX) return;
      if (!dragged) { lockWorkColWidths(table); dragged = true; }
      setWorkColWidth(table, index, key, startW + (startX - ev.clientX));
    };
    const up = () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousemove', move); document.removeEventListener('mouseup', up);
      document.body.classList.remove('col-resizing');
      if (dragged) saveWorkWidths();
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousemove', move);
    document.addEventListener('mouseup', up);
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('click', e => { if (resizeHandle(e)) { e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault(); } }, true);
  document.addEventListener('dblclick', e => {
    const h = resizeHandle(e);
    if (!h) return;
    e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault();
    const table = h.closest('table'), kind = table.dataset.widthGroup;
    loadWorkWidths()[kind] = {};
    const columns = workColumns(kind);
    table.style.width = columns.width;
    table.querySelector('colgroup').innerHTML = columns.cols;
    saveWorkWidths();
  }, true);
  function loadPins() {
    if (pinsCache) return pinsCache;
    pinsCache = new Set();
    try {
      const saved = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem(PINS_KEY) || '[]');
      if (Array.isArray(saved)) saved.forEach(tz => pinsCache.add(String(tz)));
    } catch (e) { /* אחסון חסום או נתון פגום: ממשיכים בלי סיכות שמורות */ }
    return pinsCache;
  }
  function savePins() {
    try { window.localStorage.setItem(PINS_KEY, JSON.stringify([...loadPins()])); } catch (e) { /* האחסון לא זמין: הסיכות תקפות לסשן הנוכחי */ }
  }
  function loadDeferred() {
    if (deferredCache) return deferredCache;
    try {
      const saved = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem(DEFERRED_KEY) || '[]');
      if (!Array.isArray(saved)) throw new Error('רשימת החניכים שהורדו אינה תקינה');
      deferredCache = new Set(saved.map(String));
      return deferredCache;
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('טעינת חניכים שהורדו נכשלה', e);
      throw e;
    }
  }

  function hideBreakdownTooltip() {
    const tip = $('bg-drawer-tooltip');
    if (tip) tip.remove();
    document.querySelectorAll('#bagrut-table .bg-drawer-required[aria-describedby]').forEach(el => el.removeAttribute('aria-describedby'));
  }

  function describeMxCell(el, add) {
    if (!el) return;
    const ids = (el.getAttribute('aria-describedby') || '').split(/\s+/).filter(id => id && id !== 'mxPop');
    if (add) ids.push('mxPop');
    if (ids.length) el.setAttribute('aria-describedby', ids.join(' '));
    else el.removeAttribute('aria-describedby');
  }

  function showBreakdownTooltip(cell) {
    hideBreakdownTooltip();
    const lines = JSON.parse(cell.dataset.breakdownLines);
    const tip = document.createElement('div');
    tip.id = 'bg-drawer-tooltip';
    tip.className = 'bg-drawer-tooltip';
    tip.setAttribute('role', 'tooltip');
    tip.innerHTML = `${lines.map(line => `<div>${E(line)}</div>`).join('')}<small>לחיצה על השורה לפירוט מלא</small>`;
    document.body.appendChild(tip);
    cell.setAttribute('aria-describedby', tip.id);
    const r = cell.getBoundingClientRect(), width = tip.offsetWidth, height = tip.offsetHeight;
    tip.style.left = `${Math.max(8, Math.min(r.left, window.innerWidth - width - 8))}px`;
    tip.style.top = `${r.bottom + height + 8 <= window.innerHeight ? r.bottom + 4 : Math.max(8, r.top - height - 4)}px`;
  }

  function renderBreakdownDrawer(app, list, signals) {
    const tz = app.state.bagrut.drawerTz;
    const row = list.find(r => r.t.tz === tz);
    if (!row) return BagrutView.bagrutDrawerClose.call(app);
    const table = $('bagrut-table'), parent = $('panel-bagrut') || table.parentElement;
    let drawer = $('bagrut-breakdown-drawer');
    if (!drawer) {
      drawer = document.createElement('aside');
      drawer.id = 'bagrut-breakdown-drawer';
      drawer.className = 'bg-drawer';
      drawer.setAttribute('role', 'dialog');
      drawer.setAttribute('aria-label', 'פירוט זכאות לחניך');
      parent.appendChild(drawer);
    }
    const options = row.t.gradeKey === 'יב' && app.state.bagrut.overviewEntry && app.state.filters.grade === 'all'
      ? finalOptions : app.state.sim;
    const breakdown = UI.buildBagrutBreakdown(row.t, row.ev, {
      className: Config.classLabel(row.t.classKey), noData: noBagrutData(signals.get(tz), options)
    });
    const cells = app._bagrutMatrixRows && app._bagrutMatrixRows.get(tz);
    if (!cells) throw new Error('חסרים נתוני שורת הזכאות למגירת החניך');
    const matrixSubjects = cells.map(cell => {
      const subject = row.ev.subjects.find(s => s.id === cell.id);
      const findings = breakdown.subjects.filter(s => cell.id === 'major'
        ? s.name === 'מגמה' || s.name.startsWith('מגמה: ')
        : subject && s.name === subject.name);
      const tags = findings.flatMap(s => s.tags);
      const count = new Set(tags.filter(tag => tag.code).map(tag => tag.code)).size;
      const gaps = [...new Set(tags.filter(tag => !tag.code).map(tag => tag.label))];
      if (count) gaps.push(`${count} ${count === 1 ? 'שאלון' : 'שאלונים'}`);
      if (cell.forecastSecondary) gaps.push(cell.forecastSecondary);
      return { label: cell.label, text: cell.text, state: cell.state, more: cell.more, gap: gaps.join(' · '),
        forecastOnly: cell.forecastOnly };
    });
    drawer.innerHTML = `<button type="button" class="bg-drawer-close" data-act="bagrutDrawerClose" aria-label="סגירת פירוט זכאות">✕</button>${UI.renderBagrutBreakdown(breakdown, { showHeader: true, showPin: true, pinned: loadPins().has(tz), showOpen360: true, matrixSubjects })}`;
    table.querySelectorAll('tr[data-act="bagrutDrawerOpen"]').forEach(el => el.classList.toggle('bg-drawer-row-active', el.dataset.tz === tz));
  }

  document.addEventListener('mouseover', event => {
    const cell = event.target.closest && event.target.closest('#bagrut-table .bg-drawer-required');
    if (cell && !cell.contains(event.relatedTarget)) showBreakdownTooltip(cell);
  });
  document.addEventListener('mouseout', event => {
    const cell = event.target.closest && event.target.closest('#bagrut-table .bg-drawer-required');
    if (cell && !cell.contains(event.relatedTarget)) hideBreakdownTooltip();
  });
  document.addEventListener('focusin', event => {
    const cell = event.target.closest && event.target.closest('#bagrut-table .bg-drawer-required');
    if (cell) showBreakdownTooltip(cell);
  });
  document.addEventListener('focusout', event => {
    if (event.target.closest && event.target.closest('#bagrut-table .bg-drawer-required')) hideBreakdownTooltip();
  });
  document.addEventListener('scroll', hideBreakdownTooltip, true);
  document.addEventListener('click', event => {
    const button = event.target.closest && event.target.closest('#bagrut-table [data-bagrut-subject-details]');
    if (!button) return;
    const row = button.closest('tr'), details = row && row.nextElementSibling;
    if (!details || details.id !== button.getAttribute('aria-controls')) throw new Error('חסרה שורת פירוט למקצוע');
    details.hidden = !details.hidden;
    button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(!details.hidden));
  });
  document.addEventListener('click', event => {
    const app = window.App;
    if (!app || app.state.tab !== 'bagrut' || !app.state.bagrut.drawerTz) return;
    const target = event.target;
    if (target.closest && (target.closest('#bagrut-breakdown-drawer') || target.closest('#bagrut-table tr[data-act="bagrutDrawerOpen"]'))) return;
    BagrutView.bagrutDrawerClose.call(app);
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
    const app = window.App;
    if (event.key === 'Escape' && app && app.state.tab === 'bagrut' && app.state.bagrut.drawerTz) {
      BagrutView.bagrutDrawerClose.call(app, true);
      event.preventDefault(); event.stopImmediatePropagation();
      return;
    }
    const row = event.target.closest && event.target.closest('tr[data-act="bagrutDrawerOpen"]');
    if (row && (event.target === row || event.target.classList.contains('bg-drawer-required')) &&
        (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ')) {
      event.preventDefault();
      row.click();
    }
  }, true);

  function signalsFor(rows, sim) {
    const adaptive = sim.fullMode && rows.some(r => r.t.gradeKey !== 'יב')
      ? SignalEngine.computeAll({ ...sim, fullMode: false }).byTz : null;
    const regular = SignalEngine.computeAll(sim).byTz;
    return new Map(rows.map(r => [r.t.tz, (r.t.gradeKey === 'יב' || !adaptive ? regular : adaptive).get(r.t.tz)]));
  }

  function signalSummary(rows, signals, sim) {
    const quality = rows.filter(({ t }) => signals.get(t.tz).ev.tier !== 5 && signals.get(t.tz).ev.manualReview.required &&
      signals.get(t.tz).dataQuality.some(f => f.id === 'data.manual_review')).length;
    const missing = [...new Set(rows.filter(r => signals.get(r.t.tz).ev.tier !== 5).map(r => r.t.gradeKey))].filter(grade => {
      const p = SignalEngine.prerequisitesFor(grade);
      return !p.hasBagrut && !(sim.useCurrent && p.hasSadin);
    });
    if (!missing.length && !quality) return '';
    const details = [
      missing.length ? `לא ניתן לקבוע — חסר קובץ בגרות לשכבות ${missing.map(Util.gl).join(', ')}` : '',
      quality ? `איכות נתונים: ${quality} חניכים נדרשים לבדיקת רכזת. הבדיקה אינה דגל סיכון פדגוגי.` : ''
    ].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
    return `<span class="badge gate-warn bagrut-quality" tabindex="0" aria-label="${E(details)}" title="${E(details)}">${quality ? `${quality} לבדיקה` : 'חסר מידע'}</span>`;
  }

  function target30(s, ev, rules, includeDirectTargets = false) {
    const missing = s.unresolved.concat(s.pending);
    const internal = missing.filter(c => c.internal);
    const allInternal = s.comps.filter(c => c.internal);
    const total = s.comps.reduce((n, c) => n + c.weight, 0);
    const internalWeight = internal.reduce((n, c) => n + c.weight, 0);
    const totalInternalWeight = allInternal.reduce((n, c) => n + c.weight, 0);
    if (!total || !internal.length || Math.abs(totalInternalWeight * 100 / total - 30) > 0.000001) return null;
    const r = s.reverse;
    const notes = [];
    if (ev.manual) notes.push('קיימת הכרעה ידנית; היעד אינו משנה אותה');
    if (ev.manualReview.required) notes.push('נדרשת החלטת רכזת');
    let target = 'לא ניתן לקבוע', directTarget = null, compensationTarget = null;
    if (!r || missing.some(c => !c.internal)) {
      notes.push('נדרשים נתונים נוספים או בדיקה של הרכיבים החסרים');
    } else {
      const recordedInternal = allInternal.filter(c => c.state === 'num').reduce((n, c) => n + c.num * c.weight, 0);
      const fullTarget = n => Math.ceil(+((recordedInternal + Math.max(1, n) * internalWeight) / totalInternalWeight).toFixed(6));
      if (r.required30 <= 100) directTarget = fullTarget(r.required30);
      if (r.requiredFor45 <= 100) compensationTarget = fullTarget(r.requiredFor45);
      const canCompensate = !!r.compensationAvailable &&
        r.requiredFor45 <= 100 && r.requiredFor45 < Math.max(1, r.required30);
      if (canCompensate) {
        target = String(fullTarget(r.requiredFor45));
        notes.push(`לקיזוז ${r.compMin}; כפוף לכללי הזכאות ולמכסת הקיזוז`);
        notes.push(r.required30 <= 100 ? `למעבר ללא קיזוז נדרש ${fullTarget(r.required30)} ב-30%` : `מעבר ללא קיזוז אינו אפשרי; מקסימום ${r.max}`);
      } else if (r.required30 <= 100) {
        target = String(fullTarget(r.required30));
        notes.push('למעבר במקצוע ללא קיזוז');
      } else {
        target = 'לא אפשרי';
        notes.push(`גם 100 אינו מספיק; מקסימום במקצוע ${r.max}`);
      }
      if (internalWeight !== totalInternalWeight) {
        const required = canCompensate ? r.requiredFor45 : r.required30;
        if (required <= 100) notes.push(`ברכיב החסר (${r.pct}% ממשקל המקצוע) נדרש ${Math.max(1, required)}`);
      }
      if (allInternal.length > 1) notes.push('היעד הוא ממוצע משוקלל של רכיבי ה-30%');
    }
    const current = s.reportedScore == null ? 'אין ציון מספרי מדווח' :
      `ממוצע הציונים המדווחים ${Util.fmt(s.reportedScore, 1)}; דווחו ${s.reportedPct}% ממשקל המקצוע`;
    return { current, target, note: notes.join(' · '), ...(includeDirectTargets ? { directTarget, compensationTarget } : {}) };
  }

  window.BagrutView = {
  bagrutSubjectChange(value) {
    if (this.state.bagrut.targets) return;
    Object.assign(this.state.bagrut, { drill: value, readinessClass: '', readinessFilter: 'all', targets: false });
    this.dirty.add('bagrut'); this.renderTab('bagrut');
  },

  mxPop(el) {
    if (this._mxPinned === el) return this.hideMxPop();
    this.showMxPop(el, true);
  },

  bagrutMode(el) {
    if (el.disabled || this.state.bagrut.targets) return;
    const fullMode = el.dataset.mode === 'final';
    this.state.bagrut.drillSim = { ...this.state.sim, ...this.state.bagrut.drillSim, fullMode, useCurrent: !fullMode };
    this.dirty.add('bagrut'); this.renderTab('bagrut');
  },

  bagrutSort(el) {
    const st = this.state.bagrut, key = el.dataset.key;
    if (st.sort === key) st.dir *= -1; else { st.sort = key; st.dir = key === 'tier' ? -1 : 1; }
    this.dirty.add('bagrut'); this.renderTab('bagrut');
  },

  bagrutPin(el) {
    if (!this.requireEdit()) return;
    if (el.dataset.defer === 'true') {
      const deferred = loadDeferred(), tz = el.dataset.tz, wasDeferred = deferred.has(tz);
      const next = new Set(deferred);
      if (wasDeferred) next.delete(tz); else next.add(tz);
      try {
        window.localStorage.setItem(DEFERRED_KEY, JSON.stringify([...next]));
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('שמירת חניכים שהורדו נכשלה', e);
        this.toast('לא ניתן לשמור את השינוי בסדר החניכים.', 'error');
        return;
      }
      deferredCache = next;
      if (!wasDeferred && this.state.bagrut.workTz === tz) {
        const ids = this._bagrutWorkIds || [], index = ids.indexOf(tz);
        this.state.bagrut.workTz = ids[index + 1] || ids[index - 1] || '';
      }
      this.dirty.add('bagrut'); this.renderTab('bagrut');
      return;
    }
    const pins = loadPins(), tz = el.dataset.tz;
    if (pins.has(tz)) pins.delete(tz); else pins.add(tz);
    savePins();
    this.dirty.add('bagrut'); this.renderTab('bagrut');
  },

  bagrutDrawerOpen(el) {
    const rows = this.dashRows(), signals = signalsFor(rows, this.state.sim);
    const list = this._bagrutLegacyList || this.bagrutList(rows, signals);
    if (!list.some(r => r.t.tz === el.dataset.tz)) {
      this.toast('החניך אינו זמין בסינון הנוכחי.', 'warn');
      return;
    }
    this.state.bagrut.drawerTz = el.dataset.tz;
    hideBreakdownTooltip();
    renderBreakdownDrawer(this, list, this._bagrutLegacySignals || signals);
  },

  bagrutDrawerClose(restoreFocus = false) {
    const tz = this.state.bagrut.drawerTz;
    this.state.bagrut.drawerTz = null;
    const drawer = $('bagrut-breakdown-drawer'), table = $('bagrut-table');
    if (drawer) drawer.remove();
    hideBreakdownTooltip();
    if (table) {
      const rows = [...table.querySelectorAll('tr[data-act="bagrutDrawerOpen"]')];
      rows.forEach(row => row.classList.remove('bg-drawer-row-active'));
      const row = restoreFocus && rows.find(el => el.dataset.tz === tz);
      if (row) row.focus();
    }
  },

  bagrutDrill(el) {
    if (this.state.bagrut.targets) return;
    Object.assign(this.state.bagrut, { drill: el.dataset.sel, readinessClass: '', readinessFilter: 'all', targets: false });
    this.dirty.add('bagrut'); this.renderTab('bagrut');
  },

  readinessFilter(el) {
    const st = this.state.bagrut;
    st.readinessFilter = st.readinessFilter === el.dataset.filter ? 'all' : el.dataset.filter;
    this.dirty.add('bagrut'); this.renderTab('bagrut');
  },

  bagrutReadinessClass(el) {
    const st = this.state.bagrut;
    st.readinessClass = st.readinessClass === el.dataset.class ? '' : el.dataset.class;
    this.dirty.add('bagrut'); this.renderTab('bagrut');
  },

  bagrutDrillBack() {
    Object.assign(this.state.bagrut, { drill: 'all', readinessClass: '', readinessFilter: 'all' });
    this.dirty.add('bagrut'); this.renderTab('bagrut');
  },

  bagrutMxSort(el) {
    const st = this.state.bagrut, key = el.dataset.key;
    if (st.mxSort === key) st.mxDir *= -1; else { st.mxSort = key; st.mxDir = 1; }
    this.dirty.add('bagrut'); this.renderTab('bagrut');
  },

  bagrutChip(el) {
    const st = this.state.bagrut, chip = el.dataset.chip;
    if (st.targets) return;
    st.chip = st.chip === chip ? 'all' : chip;
    st.overviewEntry = false;
    this.dirty.add('bagrut'); this.renderTab('bagrut');
  },

  bagrutTargets() {
    const st = this.state.bagrut;
    st.targets = !st.targets;
    if (st.targets) {
      st.targetPreviousChip = st.chip;
      Object.assign(st, { drill: 'all', readinessClass: '', readinessFilter: 'all' });
    } else {
      st.chip = st.targetPreviousChip;
      delete st.targetPreviousChip;
    }
    this.dirty.add('bagrut'); this.renderTab('bagrut');
  },

  bagrutTargetSort(el) {
    const st = this.state.bagrut, key = el.dataset.key;
    if (st.targetSort === key) st.targetDir *= -1; else { st.targetSort = key; st.targetDir = 1; }
    this.dirty.add('bagrut'); this.renderTab('bagrut');
  },

  target30Rows(list) {
    const rules = Config.rulesForGrade('יב'), result = [];
    list.filter(({ t }) => t.gradeKey === 'יב').forEach(({ t }) => {
      const ev = BagrutEngine.evaluate(t.tz, { fullMode: true });
      ev.subjects.forEach(s => {
        const target = target30(s, ev, rules);
        if (target) result.push({ t, subject: s.name, ...target });
      });
    });
    return result;
  },

  exportTargets30() {
    if (this.state.filters.grade !== 'יב') {
      this.toast('כדי לייצא ציוני 30% יש לבחור בשכבת י״ב.', 'warn');
      return;
    }
    if (!SignalEngine.prerequisitesFor('יב').hasBagrut) {
      this.toast('לא ניתן לייצא: חסר קובץ בגרות לשכבת י"ב', 'error');
      return;
    }
    const roster = this.filtered().filter(t => t.gradeKey === 'יב');
    const st = this.state.bagrut;
    const sim100 = !!st.sim100 && st.drill === 'all' && !st.targets;
    const options = { ...finalOptions, ignoreInternals: !!st.ignoreInternals, ...(sim100 ? { sim100: true } : {}) };
    const signals = signalsFor(roster.map(t => ({ t })), options);
    const rows = roster.map(t => ({ t, ev: signals.get(t.tz).ev }));
    const overviewSource = st.overviewEntry && !st.targets && !sim100;
    const source = overviewSource ? roster.map(t => ({ t, ev: this.getEval(t.tz) })) : rows;
    const selected = this.filterByChip(source, st.targets ? 'all' : st.chip, overviewSource ? undefined : signals);
    const targets = BagrutView.target30Rows.call(this, selected);
    if (!targets.length) {
      this.toast('אין יעדי 30% לייצוא בסינון הנוכחי', 'warn');
      return;
    }
    const quote = value => `"${String(value).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
    const safe = value => {
      const text = String(value == null ? '' : value);
      return quote(/^[\t\r\n]|^[\s\u0000-\u001f]*[=+\-@]/.test(text) ? `'${text}` : text);
    };
    const id = value => /^\d+$/.test(value) ? quote(`="${value}"`) : safe(value);
    const header = ['ת"ז', 'שם חניך', 'כיתה', 'מקצוע', 'מצב/ציון נוכחי', 'יעד מינימלי ב-30%', 'הערה'];
    const lines = [header.map(safe).join(','), ...targets.map(r =>
      [id(r.t.tz), ...[r.t.name, Config.classLabel(r.t.classKey), r.subject, r.current, r.target, r.note].map(safe)].join(','))];
    const blob = new Blob(['\ufeff', lines.join('\r\n'), '\r\n'], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' });
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.download = `bagrut-target-30-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.csv`;
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 1000);
  },

  bagrutScope(el) {
    if (!this.state.bagrut.targets) this.state.bagrut.chip = 'all';
    this.applyBagrutScope(el.dataset.scope);
    this.alertOut = [];
    this.renderTab(this.state.tab);
  },

  bagrutOnlyPending(el) {
    this.state.bagrut.onlyPending = el.checked;
    this.dirty.add('bagrut'); this.renderTab('bagrut');
  },

  subjectReadinessModel(sel, trainees, onlyPending = false) {
    const [kind, id] = String(sel).split(':');
    const questionnaires = new Map(), all = [], groupsOn = !!Store.loaded.groups;
    let name = '', pass = Number(Config.rules().passGrade) || 55;
    const grades = [...new Set(trainees.map(t => t.gradeKey))];
    (grades.length ? grades : ['יב']).forEach(grade => Config.withRulesForGrade(grade, () => {
      const rules = Config.rules();
      const def = (kind === 'major' ? rules.majors : GroupAnalyzer.coreSubjects()).find(s => s.id === id && s.enabled !== false);
      const matrix = BagrutAdvisor.subjectMatrix(sel, trainees.filter(t => t.gradeKey === grade));
      if (!def || !matrix) return;
      name = matrix.name; pass = Number(rules.passGrade) || 55;
      matrix.questionnaires.forEach(q => { if (!questionnaires.has(q.key)) questionnaires.set(q.key, q); });
      const sheet = new Map((Store.loaded.grades ? GroupAnalyzer.sadinSheet(matrix.rows.map(r => r.t), {}) : []).map(r => [r.t.tz, r]));
      matrix.rows.forEach(r => {
        const comps = kind === 'major'
          ? (def.codeMode === 'any' ? [{ codes: def.codes, expectedGrade: def.expectedGrade }] : def.codes.map(code => ({ codes: [code], expectedGrade: def.expectedGrade })))
          : (def.variableUnits ? def.levels[r.level] : def.components) || [];
        const active = comps.filter(c => c.enabled !== false);
        const passed = c => {
          const cell = r.cells[c.codes.join(',')];
          return cell && (cell.state === 'pass' || (cell.state === 'num' && cell.num >= pass));
        };
        const dueNow = active.some(c => {
          const expected = c.expectedGrade || (rules.questionnaires[String(c.codes[0])] || {}).expectedGrade;
          return Util.normGrade(expected) === grade && !passed(c);
        });
        const finalPassed = r.finalScore != null ? r.finalScore >= pass : active.length > 0 && active.every(passed);
        const pending = !finalPassed || dueNow;
        const sr = sheet.get(r.t.tz);
        const subjects = sr ? [...sr.subjects.values()].filter(s => kind === 'major'
          ? Util.matchKeywords(s.name, def.keywords)
          : (GroupAnalyzer.coreOf(s.name) || {}).id === id || s.name === GroupAnalyzer.sadinSubject(def.name)) : [];
        // מגמות אינן מנורמלות בסדין; מאחדים עליאסים לפי אותן מילות מפתח של כללי הבגרות.
        const avg = subjects.length ? Store.weightedAverage(subjects.flatMap(s => s.events)).avg : null;
        // שאלוני 30% יושבים בכספת י"ב; מי שלומד את המקצוע השנה מזוהה לפי שיבוץ בפועל ולא לפי expectedGrade.
        const fileGroup = groupsOn ? Store.groupsOf(r.t.tz).find(g => g.inFile && !g.reinforcement && (kind === 'major'
          ? Util.matchKeywords(g.subject, def.keywords)
          : (GroupAnalyzer.coreOf(g.subject) || {}).id === id || Util.matchKeywords(g.subject, def.keywords))) : null;
        const unitGroup = (fileGroup && fileGroup.groupId) || r.groupId || '';
        const enrolled = !groupsOn || !!unitGroup || avg != null;
        all.push({ ...r, pass, pending, enrolled, unitGroup, currentAvg: avg, missed: subjects.reduce((n, s) => n + s.missed.size, 0) });
      });
    }));
    // יחידה מדווחת רק כשלחלק ממשי ממנה יש ציון שוטף (25% ולפחות 2, או כולם ביחידה של 1–2), כדי שציון בודד לא יהפוך את כל השאר ל"ללא ציון".
    const units = new Map();
    const tally = (key, r) => {
      const u = units.get(key) || { size: 0, graded: 0 };
      u.size++; if (r.currentAvg != null) u.graded++;
      units.set(key, u);
    };
    all.filter(r => r.enrolled).forEach(r => {
      r.unit = r.unitGroup ? `g:${r.unitGroup}` : groupsOn ? '' : `c:${r.t.classKey || ''}`;
      if (r.unitGroup) tally(r.unit, r);
      // נפילת חירום לכיתת אם (רק ללא קובץ קבוצות) נמדדת על כל חניכי הכיתה במקצוע.
      if (!groupsOn) tally(`c:${r.t.classKey || ''}`, r);
    });
    const isReported = u => !!u && u.graded >= Math.min(u.size, Math.max(2, Math.ceil(u.size * 0.25)));
    all.forEach(r => {
      const avg = r.currentAvg;
      r.readiness = !r.enrolled ? 'notEnrolled'
        : avg != null ? (avg < r.pass ? 'fail' : avg < 65 ? 'borderline' : 'ok')
        : isReported(units.get(r.unit)) ? 'noGrade' : 'unreported';
    });
    const rows = all.filter(r => (!onlyPending || r.pending) && !(onlyPending && groupsOn && !r.enrolled));
    const summarize = members => {
      const count = s => members.filter(r => r.readiness === s).length;
      const fail = count('fail'), borderline = count('borderline'), noGrade = count('noGrade'), ok = count('ok'), unreported = count('unreported'), notEnrolled = count('notEnrolled');
      const gradedRows = members.filter(r => r.currentAvg != null), total = members.length - notEnrolled, graded = gradedRows.length;
      const assessed = total - unreported, offTrack = fail + borderline + noGrade;
      return { total, assessed, graded, fail, borderline, noGrade, ok, unreported, notEnrolled, offTrack,
        offTrackPct: assessed ? Math.round(offTrack / assessed * 100) : 0,
        coveragePct: total ? Math.round(graded / total * 100) : 0,
        avg: graded ? gradedRows.reduce((n, r) => n + r.currentAvg, 0) / graded : null };
    };
    const byClass = new Map();
    rows.forEach(r => {
      const key = r.t.classKey || '';
      if (!byClass.has(key)) byClass.set(key, []);
      byClass.get(key).push(r);
    });
    const classes = [...byClass].map(([key, members]) => {
      const s = summarize(members);
      // כיתה נכנסת לדירוג רק כשהיא מדווחת ממשית, כדי שציון 0 בודד לא יקבע "כיתה חלשה".
      const reported = s.graded >= 2 || (s.graded > 0 && s.graded === s.total);
      return { key, ...s, offTrackCount: s.offTrack, failCount: s.fail, borderlineCount: s.borderline, noGradeCount: s.noGrade, unreportedOnly: !reported };
    }).filter(c => c.total > 0).sort((a, b) => a.unreportedOnly - b.unreportedOnly || b.offTrackPct - a.offTrackPct || (a.avg ?? -1) - (b.avg ?? -1) || b.offTrackCount - a.offTrackCount || natural(a.key, b.key));
    return { sel, name, pass, questionnaires: [...questionnaires.values()], rows, ...summarize(rows), classes, weakest: classes.find(c => !c.unreportedOnly) || null };
  },

  readinessBreakdown(m) {
    return `${m.fail} חניכים נכשלים · ${m.borderline} חניכים גבוליים · ${m.noGrade} חניכים ללא ציון${m.unreported ? ` · ${m.unreported} חניכים טרם דווחו` : ''}`;
  },

  readinessCompact(r) {
    return `${r.fail} נכשלים · ${r.borderline} גבוליים${r.noGrade > 0 ? ` · ${r.noGrade} ללא ציון` : ''}`;
  },

  readinessAverage(avg) { return avg == null ? '—' : Util.fmt(avg, 1); },

  workSelect(el) {
    this.state.bagrut.workTz = el.dataset.tz;
    this.dirty.add('bagrut'); this.renderTab('bagrut');
  },

  workStep(el) {
    const ids = this._bagrutWorkIds || [], index = ids.indexOf(this.state.bagrut.workTz);
    if (index < 0) return;
    const next = index + Number(el.dataset.step);
    if (next < 0 || next >= ids.length) return;
    this.state.bagrut.workTz = ids[next];
    this.dirty.add('bagrut'); this.renderTab('bagrut');
  },

  workGrade12() {
    Object.assign(this.state.bagrut, { chip: 'all', drill: 'all', targets: false, overviewEntry: false, whatifEntry: false, workTz: '' });
    this.changeGradeFilter('יב');
  },

  renderBagrutWorklist() {
    const st = this.state.bagrut, roster = this.filtered().filter(t => t.gradeKey === 'יב');
    const listColumns = workColumns('list'), subjectColumns = workColumns('subjects');
    const options = { ...finalOptions, ignoreInternals: !!st.ignoreInternals };
    const actualSignals = signalsFor(roster.map(t => ({ t })), options);
    const signalMap = st.sim100 ? signalsFor(roster.map(t => ({ t })), { ...options, sim100: true }) : actualSignals;
    const rows = roster.map(t => ({ t, ev: signalMap.get(t.tz).ev }));
    const rules = Config.rulesForGrade('יב');
    const offsetCache = new WeakMap();
    const offsetInfo = ev => {
      if (offsetCache.has(ev)) return offsetCache.get(ev);
      const failures = ev.gates[1] === 'off' ? [] : ev.subjects.filter(s => {
        if (s.exempt || s.comps.some(c => !c.internal && (c.state === 'missing' || c.failed || c.blocked))) return false;
        const missing = s.unresolved.concat(s.pending);
        return (!missing.length && s.adj != null && Math.round(s.adj) < rules.passGrade) ||
          (missing.length > 0 && missing.every(c => c.internal && !c.failed && !c.blocked) &&
            s.reverse && s.reverse.required30 > 100) ||
          (st.sim100 && missing.some(c => c.internal && (c.failed || c.blocked)));
      });
      const majorBlock = ev.blockers.find(b => b.gate === 2 && b.k === 'major-fail');
      const failedMajor = majorBlock && ev.major.cands.find(c => majorBlock.text.includes(c.name));
      if (failedMajor && !failures.some(s => s.id === failedMajor.coreId)) failures.push(failedMajor);
      const single = failures.length === 1 ? failures[0] : null;
      const applied = single && single.compApplied && !single.critical ? single : null;
      const pending = applied && (ev.subjects.some(s => s !== applied && !s.exempt &&
        s.unresolved.concat(s.pending).some(c => !c.failed && !c.blocked)) ||
        ev.blockers.some(b => b.gate === 2 && ['major-missing', 'major-none'].includes(b.k)));
      const complete = single && single.comps && !single.unresolved.length && !single.pending.length;
      const rejected = failures.length > 1 || !!(single && (single.critical || !single.comps || (complete && !applied)));
      const result = { failures, applied, pending, rejected };
      offsetCache.set(ev, result);
      return result;
    };
    const known = rows.filter(r => !noBagrutData(signalMap.get(r.t.tz), options) && r.ev.tier !== 0 && r.ev.tier !== 5);
    const forecastOptions = { ...options, fullMode: false, useCurrent: true };
    const forecast = signalsFor(known, forecastOptions);
    const eligible = ev => (ev.tier === 1 || ev.tier === 2) &&
      (ev.manual || (!offsetInfo(ev).rejected && !offsetInfo(ev).pending));
    const todayCount = known.filter(r => eligible(r.ev)).length;
    const forecastCount = known.filter(r => [1, 2].includes(forecast.get(r.t.tz).ev.tier)).length;
    const metric = (count, label) => `<span><strong>${known.length ? `${Math.round(count / known.length * 100)}%` : '—'}</strong> ${label}: ${count} מתוך ${known.length}</span>`;
    const globalRows = roster.map(t => ({ t, ev: this.getEval(t.tz) }));
    const overviewIds = st.overviewEntry && !st.sim100 ? new Set(this.filterByChip(globalRows, st.chip).map(r => r.t.tz)) : null;
    const attention = st.overviewEntry && st.chip === 'attention' ? this.attentionModel(roster, globalRows) : null;
    const matches = (r, chip) => chip === 'eligible' ? eligible(r.ev) : this.chipMatch(r, chip, signalMap.get(r.t.tz));
    const selected = rows.filter(r => overviewIds ? overviewIds.has(r.t.tz) : matches(r, st.chip));
    const order = r => r.ev.tier === 5 ? 0 : r.ev.tier === 0 || r.ev.manualReview.required || r.ev.tier >= 3 || !eligible(r.ev) ? 2 : 1;
    selected.sort((a, b) => order(b) - order(a) || b.ev.tier - a.ev.tier || natural(a.t.name, b.t.name) || natural(a.t.tz, b.t.tz));
    const deferred = loadDeferred(), active = selected.filter(r => !deferred.has(r.t.tz)), lowered = selected.filter(r => deferred.has(r.t.tz));
    this._bagrutWorkIds = active.map(r => r.t.tz);
    if (!selected.some(r => r.t.tz === st.workTz)) st.workTz = active.length ? active[0].t.tz : '';
    const quick = (id, label) => `<button type="button" class="gstat" data-act="bagrutChip" data-chip="${id}" aria-pressed="${st.chip === id}"><span>${label}</span><span>${rows.filter(r => matches(r, id)).length}</span></button>`;
    setHtml('bagrut-kpis', `<div class="card bagrut-work-summary">
      <div class="bagrut-work-metrics">${metric(todayCount, st.sim100 ? 'עומדים בתנאי סוף י״ב בסימולציה' : 'עומדים היום בתנאי סוף י״ב')}${metric(forecastCount, 'צפי לסוף השנה')}</div>
      <p>מתוך חניכי י״ב בסינון עם נתוני בגרות, כולל חנ״מ והכרעות ידניות; ללא מסלול 12 שנות לימוד. ${st.ignoreInternals ? 'הבדיקה מתעלמת מיחידות פנימיות ואינה קביעה סופית. ' : ''}הצפי מותאם לשלב בשנה ומשלב ציונים שוטפים; רכיבי י״ב שטרם דווחו בהיקף הנדרש בשכבה אינם נחשבים חסרים. הצפי אינו הבטחה לזכאות.</p>
      ${st.overviewEntry ? '<p>הרשימה שומרת על החניכים שנבחרו בחדר הבקרה; מצב כל חניך נבדק כאן לפי סוף י״ב.</p>' : ''}
      ${attention ? `<p>במעקב: ${attention.tzSet.size} חניכים · ${attention.bagrutRisk.length} בסיכון בבגרות · ${attention.multi.length} עם 2+ שליליים/חסרים בשוטף${attention.both ? ` (${attention.both} בשניהם)` : ''}</p>` : ''}
      <div class="bagrut-tabs" role="group" aria-label="סינון רשימת העבודה">${quick('all', 'הכל')}${quick('risk', 'בסיכון')}${quick('eligible', 'עומדים בתנאים')}${quick('pinned', 'במעקב צמוד')}</div></div>`);
    const kpis = $('bagrut-kpis');
