      if (s.variableUnits) Object.keys(s.levels || {}).sort().forEach(l => targets.push({ v: `core:${s.id}:${l}`, l: `${s.name} ${l} יח'` }));
      else targets.push({ v: `core:${s.id}:`, l: `${s.name} (${s.units || 2} יח')` });
    });
    r.majors.forEach(m => targets.push({ v: `major:${m.id}`, l: `מגמה: ${m.name}` }));
    r.internals.forEach(i => targets.push({ v: `internal:${i.id}`, l: `יחידה פנימית: ${i.name}` }));
    targets.push({ v: 'immigrant', l: 'שאלון עולים / מותאם' });
    const subs = this.calibModel().filter(x => x.loc.t === 'core' && !x.sub);
    const cur = row ? { code: row.code, name: row.name, target: row.targetKey, weight: row.weight, internal: row.internal ? '1' : '', sub: row.sub ? `${row.loc.id}|${row.loc.level}|${row.loc.ci}` : '' }
      : { code: preset.code || '', name: preset.name || '', target: targets[0].v, weight: 100, internal: '', sub: '' };
    const sel = (key, opts, val) => `<select class="inp" data-f="${key}">${opts.map(o => `<option value="${E(o.v)}" ${o.v === val ? 'selected' : ''}>${E(o.l)}</option>`).join('')}</select>`;
    this.formCtx = { onSubmit: values => this.calibSave(values, row) };
    this.openModal(`
      <h3 class="text-lg font-semibold mb-3">${row ? `עריכת שאלון ${E(row.code)}` : 'הוספת סמל שאלון'}</h3>
      <div class="grid gap-3 text-sm">
        <label class="flex flex-col gap-1">סמל שאלון<input class="inp font-mono" data-f="code" value="${E(cur.code)}" ${row ? 'readonly' : ''}></label>
        <label class="flex flex-col gap-1">שם שאלון<input class="inp" data-f="name" value="${E(cur.name)}"></label>
        <label class="flex flex-col gap-1">מקצוע ורמה${sel('target', targets, cur.target)}</label>
        <label class="flex flex-col gap-1">תחליף לשאלון (עולים / מותאם) – אם נבחר, גובר על המקצוע שלמעלה${sel('sub', [{ v: '', l: '— שאלון עצמאי (לא תחליף) —' }, ...subs.map(s => ({ v: `${s.loc.id}|${s.loc.level}|${s.loc.ci}`, l: `${s.code} · ${s.group}` }))], cur.sub)}</label>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <label class="flex flex-col gap-1">משקל (%)<input class="inp" type="number" min="0" max="100" data-f="weight" value="${cur.weight ?? ''}"></label>
          <label class="flex flex-col gap-1">רכיב${sel('internal', [{ v: '', l: 'בחינה חיצונית' }, { v: '1', l: 'הערכה פנימית (בית-ספרי)' }], cur.internal)}</label>
        </div>
        <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">משקל ורכיב חלים על שאלונים בליבה; מגמות, יחידות פנימיות ושאלוני עולים נקבעים לפי הקבוצה.</p>
      </div>
      <div id="formErr" class="text-sm text-red-600 mt-2" role="alert"></div>
      <div class="flex gap-2 mt-4"><button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" data-act="formSubmit">שמירה</button><button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost" data-act="closeModal">ביטול</button></div>`);
  },

  calibDetach(r, code) {
    const strip = list => { for (let i = list.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { list[i].codes = (list[i].codes || []).filter(c => String(c) !== code); if (!list[i].codes.length) list.splice(i, 1); } };
    r.coreSubjects.forEach(s => { if (s.variableUnits) Object.values(s.levels || {}).forEach(strip); else strip(s.components || (s.components = [])); });
    r.majors.forEach(m => { m.codes = m.codes.filter(c => String(c) !== code); if (m.weights) delete m.weights[code]; });
    r.internals.forEach(i => { i.codes = i.codes.filter(c => String(c) !== code); });
    if (r.exceptionDetection && r.exceptionDetection.immigrantCodes) r.exceptionDetection.immigrantCodes = r.exceptionDetection.immigrantCodes.filter(c => String(c) !== code);
  },

  calibSave(v, row) {
    const code = String(v.code || '').trim(), name = String(v.name || '').trim();
    if (!code) return { ok: false, error: 'יש להזין סמל שאלון' };
    if (!/^[\w\u0590-\u05FF-]+$/.test(code)) return { ok: false, error: 'סמל שאלון יכול להכיל אותיות, ספרות ומקף בלבד' };
    const weight = Number(v.weight);
    const isCore = v.target.startsWith('core:') || v.sub;
    if (isCore && !(weight >= 0 && weight <= 100)) return { ok: false, error: 'משקל חייב להיות בין 0 ל-100' };
    if (!row && this.calibModel().some(x => x.code === code)) return { ok: false, error: `הסמל ${code} כבר קיים בטבלה – ערכו אותו במקום להוסיף` };
    const res = Config.updateRules(r => {
      const sameSpot = row && row.targetKey === v.target && !!row.sub === !!v.sub;
      const findList = (id, level) => {
        const s = r.coreSubjects.find(x => x.id === id);
        if (!s) throw new Error('מקצוע לא נמצא');
        return s.variableUnits ? (s.levels[level] || (s.levels[level] = [])) : (s.components || (s.components = []));
      };
      if (sameSpot && row.loc.t === 'core') {
        const comp = findList(row.loc.id, row.loc.level)[row.loc.ci];
        if (comp) { comp.weight = weight; comp.internal = !!v.internal; }
      } else {
        if (row) this.calibDetach(r, row.code);
        if (v.sub) {
          const [id, level, ci] = v.sub.split('|');
          const comp = findList(id, level)[Number(ci)];
          if (!comp) throw new Error('השאלון הראשי לא נמצא');
          comp.codes.push(code);
        } else if (v.target.startsWith('core:')) {
          const [, id, level] = v.target.split(':');
          const comp = { enabled: true, codes: [code], weight };
          if (v.internal) comp.internal = true;
          findList(id, level).push(comp);
        } else if (v.target.startsWith('major:')) {
          const m = r.majors.find(x => x.id === v.target.slice(6));
          m.codes.push(code);
        } else if (v.target.startsWith('internal:')) {
          r.internals.find(x => x.id === v.target.slice(9)).codes.push(code);
        } else {
          r.exceptionDetection = r.exceptionDetection || {};
          r.exceptionDetection.immigrantCodes = [...(r.exceptionDetection.immigrantCodes || []), code];
        }
      }
      r.questionnaires = r.questionnaires || {};
      const old = r.questionnaires[code] || {};
      r.questionnaires[code] = { ...old, name: name || old.name || '', expectedGrade: old.expectedGrade || 'יב' };
    });
    if (res.ok === false) return res;
    this.afterRulesEdit(res);
    return { ok: true };
  },

  afterRulesEdit(res) {
    this.toast(res.warnings && res.warnings.length ? `נשמר. ${res.warnings[0]}` : 'השאלון נשמר והזכאות חושבה מחדש', res.warnings && res.warnings.length ? 'warn' : 'ok');
    this.dirty.add('settings');
    this.schedule(() => { this.invalidateAll(); this.renderCalibration(); });
  },

  async cloudSave() {
    try {
      this.cloudService().saveSettings({ url: $('cloudUrl').value, clientId: $('cloudClientId').value, passcode: $('cloudPass').value });
      this.renderSync();
      this.toast('הגדרות הענן נשמרו', 'ok');
      await this.syncCloudAccess();
    } catch (e) { this.toast(`שמירת הגדרות הענן נכשלה: ${e.message}`, 'error'); }
  },
  async cloudPull() {
    if (!this.cloudService().url()) return this.toast('לא הוגדרה כתובת סנכרון ענן', 'warn');
    if (this.cloudService().hasUnpublished() && !this.confirmAction('יש שינויים שלא סונכרנו. משיכה מהענן תדרוס אותם. להמשיך?')) return;
    const r = await this.cloudRefresh(() => this.cloudService().pullCloudState({ force: true }));
    this.completeCloudAccess(r);
    recordCloudLog(this.cloudService(), 'pull', !r ? 'error' : r === 'kept-local' ? 'conflict' : 'ok');
    if (r === 'kept-local') this.toast('בוצעו שינויים בזמן המשיכה; הם נשמרו. יש לפתור את ההתנגשות לפני סנכרון.', 'warn');
    else if (r) this.toast(r === 'applied' ? 'הנתונים נטענו מהענן' : 'אין עדיין נתונים בענן', r === 'applied' ? 'ok' : 'info');
  },
  async cloudSync() {
    let attempted = false, outcome = 'error';
    try {
      this.cloudService().saveSettings({ url: $('cloudUrl').value, clientId: $('cloudClientId').value, passcode: $('cloudPass').value });
      if (!this.cloudService().url()) return this.toast('יש להגדיר כתובת סנכרון ענן בהגדרות', 'warn');
      if (!this.canEdit() || this.cloudService().hasUnpublished()) {
        await this.syncCloudAccess();
        if (!this._cloudWriteReady) return;
      }
      attempted = true;
      const r = await this.cloudRefresh(() => this.cloudService().pullCloudState());
      if (!r) return;
      if (r === 'kept-local') { outcome = 'conflict'; return; }
      const pushed = await this.cloudService().pushCloudState();
      if (pushed === 'kept-local') { outcome = 'conflict'; return; }
      outcome = 'ok';
      this.toast('הנתונים סונכרנו לענן', 'ok');
    } catch (e) { this.toast(`הסנכרון נכשל: ${e.message}`, 'error'); }
    finally {
      if (attempted) recordCloudLog(this.cloudService(), 'sync', outcome);
      this.renderSync();
    }
  },
  async cloudForcePush() {
    if (!this.confirmAction('דחיפה כפויה תחליף את נתוני הענן בנתונים המקומיים ותשפיע על כל הצוות. מומלץ לייצא גיבוי מקומי קודם. להמשיך?')) return;
    const r = await this.cloudRefresh(() => this.cloudService().pushCloudState({ force: true }));
    this.completeCloudAccess(r);
    recordCloudLog(this.cloudService(), 'force', !r ? 'error' : r === 'kept-local' ? 'conflict' : 'ok');
    if (r && r !== 'kept-local') this.toast('הנתונים המקומיים נשלחו לענן', 'ok');
  },
  async cloudJoinLink() {
    try {
      this.cloudService().saveSettings({ url: $('cloudUrl').value, clientId: $('cloudClientId').value, passcode: $('cloudPass').value });
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(this.cloudService().teamJoinLink());
      this.toast('קישור ההצטרפות הועתק. יש לשתף רק בערוץ פרטי עם הצוות.', 'ok');
    } catch (e) { this.toast(`העתקת הקישור נכשלה: ${e.message}`, 'error'); }
  },
  async backupExport() {
    try { await this.cloudService().exportBackup(); }
    catch (e) { this.toast(`ייצוא הגיבוי נכשל: ${e.message}`, 'error'); }
  },
  cloudGoogleSignOut() {
    this.cloudService().signOutGoogle();
    this.renderSync();
    this.renderCloudCard();
  },
  async cloudWipeLocal() {
    if (!this.confirmAction('פעולה זו מנתקת את המחשב הזה בלבד ומוחקת ממנו את הנתונים ואת קוד הגישה, כדי שמשתמשים אחרים במחשב המשותף לא יוכלו לראות פרטי תלמידים.\n\nהנתונים של בית הספר בענן לא יימחקו ויישארו שמורים.\n\nשינויים שעדיין לא נשמרו בענן יאבדו, והדף ייטען מחדש. להמשיך?')) return;
    try { await this.cloudService().wipeLocalSession(); }
    catch (e) { this.toast(`מחיקת הנתונים המקומיים נכשלה: ${e.message}. הסנכרון נשאר חסום; יש לנסות למחוק שוב.`, 'error'); }
  },
  calibAdd() { this.openCalibModal(null); },
  calibAssign(el) { const u = this._calibUnk[Number(el.dataset.i)]; if (u) this.openCalibModal(null, { code: u.code, name: u.subject }); },
  calibEdit(el) { const r = this._calibRows[Number(el.dataset.i)]; if (r) this.openCalibModal(r); },
  calibDelete(el) {
    const row = this._calibRows[Number(el.dataset.i)];
    if (!row || !this.confirmAction(`למחוק את הסמל ${row.code} מ-${row.group}?`)) return;
    const res = Config.updateRules(r => {
      this.calibDetach(r, row.code);
      if (r.questionnaires) delete r.questionnaires[row.code];
    });
    if (res.ok === false) return this.toast(res.error, 'error');
    this.afterRulesEdit(res);
  },
  settingsView(el) { this.state.settingsView = el.dataset.view; this.renderSettingsNav(); },
  ovPick(el) { this.ovSelect(el.dataset.tz); this.ovCloseResults(); },
  ovSave() {
    const tz = this.ovTz;
    if (!tz) return this.toast('יש לבחור חניך מהרשימה', 'warn');
    const home = $('ovHomeroom').value === 'other' ? $('ovOtherHomeroom').value.trim() : $('ovHomeroom').value;
    if ($('ovHomeroom').value === 'other' && !home) return this.toast('יש להזין כיתת אם חדשה', 'warn');
    const res = Config.setRosterOverride(tz, {
      grade: $('ovGrade').value, homeroom: home, track: $('ovTrack').value,
      hidden: $('ovHidden').checked, note: $('ovNote').value, name: $('ovName').value
    });
    if (res.ok === false) return this.toast(res.error || 'שמירת הדריסה נכשלה', 'error');
    ['overrideTraineeSearch', 'ovGrade', 'ovHomeroom', 'ovOtherHomeroom', 'ovTrack', 'ovNote', 'ovName'].forEach(id => setVal(id, ''));
    $('ovOtherHomeroom').classList.add('hidden');
    setChecked('ovHidden', false);
    this.ovTz = '';
    this.toast(res.removed ? 'הדריסה זהה לשיוך שבקובץ ולכן לא נשמרה' : 'הדריסה נשמרה והופעלה בכל הפאנלים', res.removed ? 'info' : 'ok');
    this.afterDataChange();
  },
  classRoster(el) { this.openClassRoster(el.dataset.class); },
  ovRemove(el) {
    const res = Config.removeRosterOverride(el.dataset.tz);
    if (res.ok === false) return this.toast(res.error || 'ביטול הדריסה נכשל', 'error');
    this.toast('הדריסה בוטלה – חזר השיוך שבקובץ', 'ok');
    this.afterDataChange();
  },
  ovClearAll() {
    const count = Object.keys(Config.getRosterOverrides()).length;
    if (!count || !this.confirmAction(`לבטל את כל ${count} דריסות השיוך הפעילות?`)) return;
    const res = Config.clearRosterOverrides();
    if (res.ok === false) return this.toast(res.error || 'ביטול הדריסות נכשל', 'error');
    this.toast('כל הדריסות בוטלו – חזרו השיוכים שבקובץ', 'ok');
    this.afterDataChange();
  },
  rulesTab(el) { this.state.rulesTab = el.dataset.sub; this.renderRulesDrawer(); },
  cohortEdit(el) {
    Config.setActiveCohort(el.dataset.id);
    ['settings'].forEach(t => this.dirty.add(t));
    this.renderTab('settings');
  },
  cohortCopy() {
    const to = Config.getActiveCohortId();
    const others = Object.values(Config.getCohortProfiles()).filter(c => c.id !== to);
    if (!others.length) return this.toast('אין מחזור אחר להעתיק ממנו', 'warn');
    this.formModal(`העתקת חוקים אל ${this.cohortName(to)}`, [
      { key: 'from', label: 'העתק חוקים ממחזור', type: 'select', value: others[0].id, options: others.map(c => ({ value: c.id, label: c.name })) }
    ], v => {
      if (!this.confirmAction(`כל חוקי הבגרות והשאלונים של ${this.cohortName(to)} יוחלפו בעותק של ${this.cohortName(v.from)}. להמשיך?`)) return { ok: false, error: 'ההעתקה בוטלה' };
      const res = Config.copyCohortRules(v.from, to);
      if (res.ok !== false) { Store.refresh(); SignalEngine.invalidate(); this.toast(`החוקים הועתקו מ-${this.cohortName(v.from)}`, 'ok'); }
      return res;
    }, 'העתקה');
  },
  cohortAdd() {
    const from = Config.getActiveCohortId();
    this.formModal('הוספת מחזור חדש', [
      { key: 'name', label: 'שם המחזור', placeholder: 'מחזור ח\' (תש"צ)' }
    ], v => {
      const res = Config.addCohort(v.name, from);
      if (res.ok) {
        Config.setActiveCohort(res.id);
        this.toast(`נוסף ${v.name.trim()} כעותק של ${this.cohortName(from)}. אפשר לשייך אליו שכבה בראש המסך.`, 'ok');
      }
      return res;
    }, 'הוספה');
  },
  ruleRemove(el) {
    const list = Config.rules()[{ core: 'coreSubjects', major: 'majors', internal: 'internals' }[el.dataset.kind]] || [];
    const c = list.find(x => x.id === el.dataset.id);
    if (c && this.confirmAction(`להסיר את "${c.name}" מכללי הזכאות?`)) this.applyRule(Config.removeClause(el.dataset.kind, el.dataset.id));
  },
  compAdd(el) {
    const { kind, id, level } = el.dataset;
    this.formModal('הוספת שאלון', [
      { key: 'codes', label: 'סמלי שאלון (חלופיים, מופרדים בפסיקים)' },
      { key: 'weight', label: 'משקל (%)', type: 'number', value: 0 },
      { key: 'label', label: 'תיאור (אופציונלי)' }
    ], v => Config.upsertComponent(kind, id, level, null, { codes: v.codes.split(',').map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean), weight: Number(v.weight), label: v.label.trim() }));
  },
  compRemove(el) { this.applyRule(Config.removeComponent(el.dataset.kind, el.dataset.id, el.dataset.level, +el.dataset.i)); },
  qRemove(el) { this.applyRule(Config.removeQuestionnaire(el.dataset.code)); },
  deleteRoster() {
    if (!this.confirmAction('למחוק את מצבת החניכים? כל הפאנלים יתרוקנו עד לטעינת מצבת חדשה.')) return;
    const res = Config.clearRoster();
    if (res && res.ok === false) return this.toast(res.error, 'error');
    Config.setRosterCleared(true);
    this.rosterReport = null;
    this.afterDataChange();
  },
  gradeCohort(el) {
    const res = Config.setGradeCohort(el.dataset.grade, el.value);
    if (res.ok === false) this.toast(res.error, 'error');
    else this.toast(`שכבת ${Util.gl(Util.GRADE_KEYS.find(k => String(Util.ord(k)) === el.dataset.grade))} נבדקת כעת לפי ${this.cohortName(el.value)}`, 'ok');
    this.renderRulesDrawer();
  },
  compEnabled(el) { this.patchComp(el, { enabled: el.checked }); },
  compCodes(el) { this.patchComp(el, { codes: el.value.split(',').map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean) }); },
  compWeight(el) { this.patchComp(el, { weight: Number(el.value) }); },
  compGrade(el) { this.patchComp(el, { expectedGrade: el.value }); this.renderRulesDrawer(); },
  /* ריק = מחיקת הדריסה וירושת ברירת המחדל של המחזור; ציונים נגזרים מחושבים מחדש מיד */
  compMagen(el) {
    const v = el.value.trim(), n = Number(v);
    if (v !== '' && !(isFinite(n) && n >= 0 && n <= 100)) { this.toast('משקל המגן חייב להיות בין 0 ל-100', 'error'); this.renderRulesDrawer(); return; }
    this.patchComp(el, { magenWeight: v === '' ? null : n });
    Store.refresh();
    SignalEngine.invalidate();
    this.renderRulesDrawer();
  },
  ruleToggle(el) { this.applyRule(Config.toggleClause(el.dataset.kind, el.dataset.id)); },
  clauseCodes(el) { this.applyRule(Config.setClauseCodes(el.dataset.kind, el.dataset.id, el.value)); },
  clauseKeywords(el) { this.applyRule(Config.patchClause(el.dataset.kind, el.dataset.id, { keywords: el.value.split(',').map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean) })); },
  internalGrade(el) { this.applyRule(Config.patchClause('internal', el.dataset.id, { expectedGrade: el.value })); },
  clauseGrade(el) { this.applyRule(Config.patchClause(el.dataset.kind, el.dataset.id, { expectedGrade: el.value })); this.renderRulesDrawer(); },
  gateToggle(el) { this.applyRule(Config.setGateEnabled(el.dataset.gate, el.checked)); },
  gateUnits(el) { this.applyRule(Config.updateRules(r => { r.gates.major.requiredUnits = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, Number(el.value) || 5)); })); },
  qName(el) { this.applyRule(Config.setQuestionnaire(el.dataset.code, { name: el.value.trim() })); },
  qGrade(el) {
    let res;
    el.dataset.code.split(',').filter(Boolean).forEach(code => { res = Config.setQuestionnaire(code, { expectedGrade: el.value }); });
    this.applyRule(res);
  },
  specialEd(el) {
    const res = Config.setSpecialEdClass(el.dataset.class, el.checked);
    if (res.ok === false) { this.afterClassSettingChange(); return this.toast(res.error || 'שמירת ההגדרה נכשלה', 'error'); }
    this.afterClassSettingChange();
    this.toast(el.checked ? `כיתה ${el.dataset.class}: חניכיה במסלול חנ"מ` : `כיתה ${el.dataset.class}: הוסרה ממסלול חנ"מ`, 'ok');
  },
  classMove(el) {
    let home = el.value;
    if (home === 'other') home = (this.promptAction('הקלידו כיתת אם חדשה (למשל יא3)') || '').trim();
    if (!home) return this.openClassRoster(el.dataset.class);
    const patch = { grade: Util.parseClass('', home).gradeKey, homeroom: home };
    this.classRosterApply(el.dataset.tz, el.dataset.class, patch, `החניך הועבר לכיתה ${home}`);
  },
  classTrack(el) {
    this.classRosterApply(el.dataset.tz, el.dataset.class, { track: el.value }, `המסלול עודכן: ${el.value}`);
  },
  renderDataTools(DATASETS) {
    const sadinGrades = new Map(), roster = Config.rosterMap();
    Config.getRows('sadinData').forEach(r => {
      const grade = r.gradeKey || (roster.get(r.tz) || {}).grade;
      if (!Util.GRADE_KEYS.includes(grade)) return;
      if (!sadinGrades.has(grade)) sadinGrades.set(grade, new Set());
      if (r.tz) sadinGrades.get(grade).add(r.tz);
    });
    const card = key => {
      const dataset = Config.getDataset(key), loaded = !!dataset;
      const files = dataset && dataset.files ? dataset.files.length : loaded && dataset.fileName ? 1 : 0;
      const perGrade = key === 'sadinData'
        ? Util.GRADE_KEYS.filter(g => sadinGrades.has(g)).map(g =>
          `<button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm w-full justify-between" data-act="clearSadinGrade" data-grade="${E(g)}" title="הסרת שורות סדין של שכבה ${E(Util.gl(g))}">הסרת שכבה ${E(Util.gl(g))} (${sadinGrades.get(g).size}) ×</button>`).join('')
        : '';
      return `<section class="card p-4 sd-source-card">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2"><h3 class="font-semibold">${E(DATASETS[key].title)}</h3>
          <details class="sd-kebab"><summary class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" aria-label="${E(`פעולות ${DATASETS[key].title}`)}">⋯</summary>
            <div class="sd-kebab-menu">
              <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm w-full" data-act="remap" data-key="${E(key)}">מיפוי עמודות</button>
              ${perGrade}
              <button type="button" class="btn btn-danger btn-sm w-full" data-act="clearPanel" data-key="${E(key)}" ${loaded ? '' : 'disabled'}>ניקוי נתונים</button>
            </div>
          </details>
        </div>
        <div data-fresh-bar="${key}" role="status"></div>
        <div class="text-xs text-slate-500">${loaded ? `${Config.getRows(key).length} שורות · ${files} קבצים` : 'טרם נטענו נתונים'}</div>
        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1">${this.coverageHtml(key)}</div>
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary w-full sd-source-upload" data-upload="${E(key)}">טעינת קובץ</button>
      </section>`;
    };
    setHtml('dataTools', Object.keys(DATASETS).map(card).join(''));
    Object.keys(DATASETS).forEach(key => {
      document.querySelectorAll(`#dataTools [data-fresh-bar="${key}"]`).forEach(el => { el.innerHTML = this.freshBarHtml(key); });
    });
  },

  async clearPanel(el, DATASETS) {
    const key = el.dataset.key, ds = DATASETS[key];
    if (!ds || !this.confirmAction(`למחוק את כל נתוני "${ds.title}" (כל השכבות)? המצבת וההגדרות יישמרו.`)) return;
    try { await Config.clearDataset(key); }
    catch (err) { return this.toast(`מחיקת הנתונים נכשלה: ${err.message}`, 'error'); }
    this.afterDataChange();
    this.toast(`נתוני "${ds.title}" נוקו`, 'ok');
  },
  async clearSadinGrade(el) {
    const grade = el.dataset.grade;
    if (!this.confirmAction(`למחוק את שורות הסדין של שכבה ${Util.gl(grade)}? שורות ללא שכבה מזוהה יישארו.`)) return;
    try {
      const res = await Config.clearSadinGrade(grade);
      if (!res.ok) return this.toast(res.error, 'error');
      this.afterDataChange();
      this.toast(`נתוני סדין של שכבה ${Util.gl(grade)} הוסרו`, 'ok');
    } catch (err) { this.toast(`מחיקת הנתונים נכשלה: ${err.message}`, 'error'); }
  },
  remap(el) { this.remapColumns(el.dataset.key); },
  bindRulesEvents() {
    on('rulesDrawer', 'click', e => {
      const button = e.target.closest('[data-core-select]');
      if (!button) return;
      this.state.rulesTab = 'core';
      this.state.selectedCoreSubjectId = button.dataset.coreSelect;
      this.renderRulesDrawer();
    });
    on('qCalibSearch', 'input', e => {
      this.state.calibSearch = e.target.value;
      RulesView.renderCalibrationTable.call(this);
    });
    document.addEventListener('click', e => {
      const target = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('details.sd-kebab');
      document.querySelectorAll('#panel-settings details.sd-kebab[open]').forEach(menu => {
        if (menu !== target || e.target.closest('[data-act]')) menu.open = false;
      });
    }, true);
    on('qualityDetails', 'click', e => {
      const filter = e.target.closest('[data-quality-filter]');
      if (filter) {
        this.state.qualityFilter = filter.dataset.qualityFilter;
        this.state.qualityExpanded = false;
        this.renderQualityCard();
        return;
      }
      if (e.target.closest('[data-quality-more]')) {
        this.state.qualityExpanded = true;
        this.renderQualityCard();
        return;
      }
      const button = e.target.closest('[data-quality-open]');
      const group = button && this._qualityGroups && this._qualityGroups[Number(button.dataset.qualityOpen)];
      if (!group) return;
      const students = new Map();
      group[1].forEach(({ signal }) => students.set(signal.tz, signal));
      const rows = [...students.values()].map(signal => {
        const trainee = Store.trainees.get(signal.tz);
        return `<li><button type="button" class="underline" data-act="openProfile" data-tz="${E(signal.tz)}">${E(trainee ? trainee.name : signal.tz)}</button><ul>${signal.dataQuality.map(flag => `<li>${E(flag.label)}${flag.detail ? `: ${E(flag.detail)}` : ''}</li>`).join('')}</ul></li>`;
      }).join('');
      this.openModal(`<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2"><h3 class="text-lg font-semibold">${E(group[0])} · ${group[1].length} רשומות</h3><button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-act="closeModal">סגירה</button></div><ul class="text-sm space-y-3 mt-3">${rows}</ul>`, true);
    });
    on('cloudBody', 'click', e => {
      const button = e.target.closest('[data-toggle-pass]');
      const input = $('cloudPass');
      if (!button || !input) return;
      const visible = input.type === 'password';
      input.type = visible ? 'text' : 'password';
      button.textContent = visible ? 'הסתר' : 'הצג';
      button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(visible));
    });
    const num = (id, fn) => on(id, 'change', e => (e.target.value === '' ? this.renderRulesDrawer() : this.applyRule(fn(Number(e.target.value)))));
    on('ruleCompEnabled', 'change', e => this.applyRule(Config.setCompensation({ enabled: e.target.checked })));
    on('ruleCompPoints', 'change', e => this.applyRule(Config.setCompensation({ pointsEnabled: e.target.checked })));
    num('ruleCompMin', v => Config.setCompensation({ min: v }));
    num('ruleCompMax', v => Config.setCompensation({ max: v }));
    num('ruleCompCount', v => Config.setCompensation({ maxCount: v }));
    on('ruleCompExcluded', 'change', e => this.applyRule(Config.setCompensation({ excludedSubjects: e.target.value.split(',') })));
    num('ruleParamPass', v => Config.setRuleParam('passGrade', v));
    num('ruleParamBorderline', v => Config.setRuleParam('borderlineMin', v));
    num('ruleParamBonus', v => Config.setRuleParam('borderlineBonus', v));
    num('ruleParamMagen', v => {
      const res = Config.setRuleParam('magenWeight', v);
      Store.refresh();
      SignalEngine.invalidate();
      return res;
    });
    on('rulesReset', 'click', () => {
      if (!this.confirmAction(`לשחזר את כללי הזכאות והמשקלים של ${this.cohortName(Config.getActiveCohortId())} לברירת המחדל?`)) return;
      const res = Config.resetRules();
      Store.refresh();
      SignalEngine.invalidate();
      this.applyRule(res);
    });

    on('ovTableSearch', 'input', e => {
      this.state.ovSearch = e.target.value;
      RulesView.renderOverridesTable.call(this);
    });
    on('ovTableFilter', 'change', e => {
      this.state.ovFilter = e.target.value;
      RulesView.renderOverridesTable.call(this);
    });
    on('ovNewBtn', 'click', () => {
      RulesView.setOvPanelOpen.call(this, true);
      setVal('overrideTraineeSearch', '');
      this.ovSelect('');
      const search = $('overrideTraineeSearch');
      if (search) search.focus();
    });
    on('ovClosePanel', 'click', () => RulesView.setOvPanelOpen.call(this, false));
    on('ovTableSection', 'click', e => {
      const edit = e.target.closest('[data-ov-edit]');
      if (edit) {
        RulesView.setOvPanelOpen.call(this, true);
        this.ovSelect(edit.dataset.ovEdit);
        this.ovCloseResults();
      } else if (e.target.closest('[data-ov-show-all]')) {
        this.state.ovShowAll = !this.state.ovShowAll;
        RulesView.renderOverridesTable.call(this);
      }
    });
    on('overrideTraineeSearch', 'focus', () => this.ovSearchResults());
    on('overrideTraineeSearch', 'input', () => { this.ovSelect(''); this.ovSearchResults(); });
    on('overrideTraineeSearch', 'keydown', e => {
      if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
        const first = $('ovResults').querySelector('button');
        if (first) { e.preventDefault(); first.focus(); }
      } else if (e.key === 'Enter' && !$('ovResults').classList.contains('hidden')) {
        const first = $('ovResults').querySelector('button');
        e.preventDefault();
        if (first) first.click();
      } else if (e.key === 'Escape') this.ovCloseResults();
    });
    on('ovSearchWrap', 'focusout', e => {
      if (!e.currentTarget.contains(e.relatedTarget)) this.ovCloseResults();
    });
    on('ovResults', 'keydown', e => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        $('overrideTraineeSearch').focus();
        this.ovCloseResults();
      }
    });
    on('ovHomeroom', 'change', e => {
      $('ovOtherHomeroom').classList.toggle('hidden', e.target.value !== 'other');
      if (e.target.value === '') setVal('ovGrade', '');
      else if (e.target.value === 'other') this.ovSyncGrade($('ovOtherHomeroom').value);
      else this.ovSyncGrade(e.target.value);
      this.ovSyncDefaultTrack(e.target.value === 'other' ? $('ovOtherHomeroom').value : e.target.value);
    });
    on('ovOtherHomeroom', 'input', e => { this.ovSyncGrade(e.target.value); this.ovSyncDefaultTrack(e.target.value); });
    on('ovTrack', 'change', () => { this.ovTrackEdited = true; });
    on('ovForm', 'submit', e => { e.preventDefault(); this.acts.ovSave.call(this); });
    on('btnClearData', 'click', async () => {
      if (!this.confirmAction('למחוק את קובצי הבגרות, הקבוצות והציונים השוטפים? המצבת וההגדרות יישמרו.')) return;
      try {
        await Config.clearAllData();
        this.afterDataChange();
        this.toast('קובצי המשנה נוקו', 'ok');
      } catch (err) {
        this.afterDataChange();
        this.toast(`מחיקת הנתונים נכשלה: ${err.message}`, 'error');
      }
    });
    on('btnResetConfig', 'click', () => {
      if (!this.confirmAction('האיפוס ימחק את כל כללי הזכאות, החריגים, הכינויים, פרופילי המיפוי ודריסות שיוך (rosterOverrides). המצבת תישאר. המחיקות יסונכרנו גם לענן אם הוגדר וישפיעו על כל הצוות. להמשיך?')) return;
      const res = Config.reset({ silent: true });
      if (res && res.ok === false) return this.toast(res.error, 'error');
      this.afterDataChange();
      this.toast('ההגדרות אופסו', 'ok');
    });

  },

  };
})();


========================================
FILE: js\views\sadinView.js
========================================
(function () {
  'use strict';
  const E = Util.esc;
  const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
  const setHtml = (id, html) => { const el = $(id); if (el) el.innerHTML = html; return el; };
  const natural = (a, b) => String(a).localeCompare(String(b), 'he', { numeric: true });
  const teachersForSubject = (roster, subject) => {
    const teachers = new Set();
    roster.forEach(t => {
      Store.groupsOf(t.tz).forEach(g => {
        if (g.subject && GroupAnalyzer.sadinSubject(g.subject) === subject && g.teacher) teachers.add(g.teacher);
      });
      Store.activeCurrentOf(t.tz).forEach(e => {
        const teacher = (Store.groups.get(e.groupId) || {}).teacher || e.teacher;
        if (e.subject && GroupAnalyzer.sadinSubject(e.subject) === subject && teacher) teachers.add(teacher);
      });
    });
    return [...teachers].sort(natural);
  };
  const confirmedEvent = (m, hasGroups, tz) => {
    if (!hasGroups) return false;
    const group = Store.groups.get(m.groupId);
    return !!group && group.inFile && !!tz && group.members.has(tz);
  };
  const confirmedMissed = (subject, hasGroups, tz) =>
    [...subject.missed.values()].filter(m => confirmedEvent(m, hasGroups, tz)).length;
  const missedLabel = count => count ? ` <span class="miss-grade">${count} הוחמצו</span>` : '';
  const prerequisitesByGrade = roster => new Map([...new Set(roster.map(t => t.gradeKey))]
    .map(grade => [grade, SignalEngine.prerequisitesFor(grade)]));
  const isSocial = name => Util.norm(name) === Util.norm('מעורבות חברתית');
  const failingSubject = s => {
    if (s.avg == null || SignalEngine.isPassingScore(s.avg, { social: isSocial(s.name) })) return false;
    const scored = s.events.filter(e => e.score != null);
    const counted = scored.some(e => e.weight > 0) ? scored.filter(e => e.weight > 0) : scored;
    return !(s.avg === 0 && counted.length && counted.every(e => e.score === 0 && s.missed.has(e.key)));
  };
  const confirmedRiskSubjects = (row, hasGroups) => Config.withRulesForGrade(row.t.gradeKey, () =>
    [...row.subjects.values()].filter(s => failingSubject(s) || confirmedMissed(s, hasGroups, row.t.tz)));
  const riskSubject = s => (s.avg != null && !SignalEngine.isPassingScore(s.avg, { social: isSocial(s.name) })) || s.missed.size > 0;
  const hasEventData = ev => ev && (ev.score != null || String(ev.scoreRaw || '').trim() !== '');
  const RISK_RULES_KEY = 'schoolDashboard.hotspots.riskRules.v1';
  const CORE_ORDER = ['math', 'english', 'hebrew', 'history', 'civics', 'literature', 'tanach'];
  const CORE_ALIASES = [
    ['מתמטיקה', 'חשבון'], ['אנגלית'], ['עברית', 'הבעה', 'לשון'],
    ['היסטוריה'], ['אזרחות'], ['ספרות'], ['תנך', 'מקרא']
  ];
  const excludedWeaknessSubject = s => [s.name, ...s.entries.map(e => e.displaySubject || e.subject)]
    .some(name => {
      const text = Util.norm(name);
      return text === 'חינוך' || /^(?:מגדר|חנג|חינוך גופני)(?:$|[\s(/-])/.test(text);
    });
  const coreRank = name => {
    const core = Config.rules().coreSubjects.find(s => Util.norm(s.name) === Util.norm(name) || Util.matchKeywords(name, s.keywords));
    const rank = core ? CORE_ORDER.indexOf(core.id) : -1;
    return rank >= 0 ? rank : CORE_ALIASES.findIndex(aliases => Util.matchKeywords(name, aliases));
  };
  const validRiskRules = rules => rules && Number.isInteger(rules.minFails) && rules.minFails >= 1 && rules.minFails <= 5 &&
    Number.isInteger(rules.minMissed) && rules.minMissed >= 0 && rules.minMissed <= 5;
  function weaknessRiskRules(app) {
    const st = app.state.sadin;
    if (st.weakRiskRules) return st.weakRiskRules;
    let raw;
    try { raw = window.localStorage.getItem(RISK_RULES_KEY); }
    catch (error) {
      console.warn('weakness risk rules storage', error);
      app.toast('לא ניתן לקרוא את הגדרת הסיכון מהאחסון; ההגדרה תקפה לסשן הנוכחי בלבד.', 'warn');
    }
    let saved = null;
    if (raw) {
      try { saved = JSON.parse(raw); }
      catch (error) {
        if (!(error instanceof SyntaxError)) throw error;
        console.warn('invalid weakness risk rules', error);
      }
      if (!validRiskRules(saved)) app.toast('הגדרת הסיכון השמורה אינה תקינה; נעשה שימוש בברירת המחדל.', 'warn');
    }
    st.weakRiskRules = validRiskRules(saved) ? { minFails: saved.minFails, minMissed: saved.minMissed } : { minFails: 1, minMissed: 1 };
    return st.weakRiskRules;
  }

  function saveWeaknessRiskRules(app, rules) {
    app.state.sadin.weakRiskRules = { ...rules };
    try { window.localStorage.setItem(RISK_RULES_KEY, JSON.stringify(app.state.sadin.weakRiskRules)); }
    catch (error) {
      console.warn('weakness risk rules storage', error);
      app.toast('לא ניתן לשמור את הגדרת הסיכון באחסון; היא תקפה לסשן הנוכחי בלבד.', 'warn');
    }
  }

  function riskPopover() {
    const stepper = (key, label, suffix) => `<div class="weak-risk-row"><span>${label}</span>
      <div class="weak-risk-stepper"><button type="button" data-act="weaknessRiskAdjust" data-key="${key}" data-step="-1" aria-label="הפחתת ${suffix}">−</button>
      <span class="weak-risk-value" data-key="${key}"></span>
      <button type="button" data-act="weaknessRiskAdjust" data-key="${key}" data-step="1" aria-label="הגדלת ${suffix}">+</button></div><span>${suffix}</span></div>`;
    return `<div id="weak-risk-popover" class="card weak-risk-popover" role="dialog" aria-labelledby="weak-risk-heading" tabindex="-1">
      <h2 id="weak-risk-heading">מתי חניך נחשב בסיכון?</h2>
      ${stepper('minFails', 'נכשל לפחות ב-', 'מקצועות')}
      ${stepper('minMissed', 'הוחמצו לו לפחות', 'הערכות')}
      <p class="weak-risk-help">מספיק שאחד התנאים יתקיים. ההגדרה חלה על כל המסך.</p>
      <div class="weak-risk-preview" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div>
      <div class="weak-risk-actions"><button type="button" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" data-act="weaknessRiskApply">החל</button>
      <button type="button" class="weak-risk-reset" data-act="weaknessRiskReset">איפוס לברירת מחדל</button></div></div>`;
  }

  function updateRiskPopover(app) {
    const pop = $('weak-risk-popover'), rules = app.state.sadin.weakRiskDraft;
    if (!pop || !rules) return;
    pop.style.transform = '';
    const rect = pop.getBoundingClientRect();
    const shift = rect.left < 8 ? 8 - rect.left : rect.right > window.innerWidth - 8 ? window.innerWidth - 8 - rect.right : 0;
    if (shift) pop.style.transform = `translateX(${shift}px)`;
    pop.querySelectorAll('.weak-risk-value').forEach(el => {
      const value = rules[el.dataset.key];
      el.textContent = el.dataset.key === 'minMissed' && value === 0 ? 'ללא' : String(value);
    });
    pop.querySelectorAll('[data-act="weaknessRiskAdjust"]').forEach(el => {
      const min = el.dataset.key === 'minFails' ? 1 : 0, value = rules[el.dataset.key];
      el.disabled = Number(el.dataset.step) < 0 ? value <= min : value >= 5;
    });
    const preview = pop.querySelector('.weak-risk-preview'), roster = app.filtered();
    if (!Store.loaded.grades && roster.length) {
      preview.textContent = 'לא ניתן לקבוע ללא נתוני ציונים שוטפים.';
      return;
    }
    const model = weaknessModel(roster, app.state.sadin, rules);
    const incomplete = [...prerequisitesByGrade(roster).values()].some(p => !p.hasSadin || (rules.minMissed > 0 && !p.hasGroups));
    preview.textContent = `בהגדרה הזו: ${model.riskyClasses} מתוך ${model.classes.length} כיתות בסיכון · ${model.riskyStudents} חניכים בסיכון בסינון${incomplete || (rules.minMissed > 0 && model.uncertain) ? ' · הנתונים עשויים להיות חלקיים' : ''}`;
  }

  function relevantSubject(s) {
    const rules = Config.rules();
    const names = [s.name, ...s.entries.map(e => e.displaySubject || e.subject)];
    const defs = [...rules.coreSubjects, ...rules.majors, ...rules.internals].filter(d => d.enabled !== false);
    return defs.some(d => names.some(name =>
      Util.matchKeywords(name, [...(d.keywords || []), ...(d.groupKeywords || []), d.name]))) ||
      s.entries.some(e => {
        const group = Store.groups.get(e.groupId);
        return group && GroupAnalyzer.isMajorGroup(group);
      });
  }

  function normalizeRow(row, early) {
    return Config.withRulesForGrade(row.t.gradeKey, () => {
      const subjects = early ? new Map([...row.subjects].filter(([, s]) => relevantSubject(s))) : row.subjects;
      return { ...row, subjects, failing: [...subjects.values()].filter(failingSubject).length,
        missed: [...subjects.values()].reduce((n, s) => n + s.missed.size, 0) };
    });
  }

  function signalSummary(roster, rows, opts) {
    const prereqs = prerequisitesByGrade(roster);
    const missing = [...prereqs].flatMap(([grade, p]) => {
      return [
        !p.hasSadin ? UI.signalBadge('sadin.unknown', `לא ניתן לקבוע — חסר קובץ סדין לשכבה ${Util.gl(grade)}`) : '',
        !p.hasGroups ? UI.signalBadge('placement.unknown', `לא ניתן לקבוע שיבוץ — חסר קובץ קבוצות לשכבה ${Util.gl(grade)}`) : ''
      ].filter(Boolean);
    });
    const all = SignalEngine.computeAll(opts), pedagogical = new Map(), quality = new Map();
    const add = (map, id, tz) => {
      if (!map.has(id)) map.set(id, new Set());
      map.get(id).add(tz);
    };
    rows.forEach(row => {
      const signal = all.byTz.get(row.t.tz);
      Config.withRulesForGrade(row.t.gradeKey, () => {
        const borderline = Number(Config.rules().borderlineMin);
        row.subjects.forEach(subject => {
          if (!failingSubject(subject)) return;
          add(pedagogical, 'sadin.fail', row.t.tz);
          if (Number.isFinite(borderline) && subject.avg >= borderline) add(pedagogical, 'sadin.borderline', row.t.tz);
        });
      });
      if (signal.pedagogical.some(f => f.id === 'sadin.missed_event') &&
          [...row.subjects.values()].some(s => confirmedMissed(s, signal.prereqs.hasGroups, row.t.tz)))
        add(pedagogical, 'sadin.missed_event', row.t.tz);
      signal.dataQuality.filter(f => f.id === 'data.sadin_not_enrolled').forEach(f => add(quality, f.id, row.t.tz));
    });
    const unmatched = GroupAnalyzer.unmatchedIds().filter(item => item.sources.includes('סדין ציונים'));
    unmatched.forEach(item => add(quality, 'data.not_in_roster', item.tz));
    const badges = map => [...map].map(([id, ids]) =>     UI.signalBadge(id, `${SignalEngine.CATALOG[id].label}: ${ids.size} חניכים`)).join(' ');
    const incompleteGroups = [...prereqs.values()].some(p => !p.hasGroups);
    return `<div class="w-full space-y-2 mt-2" aria-label="דגלי ציונים ואיכות נתונים">
      ${missing.length ? `<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1">${missing.join(' ')}</div>` : ''}
      ${incompleteGroups ? '<p class="text-xs text-slate-500">החמצות דורשות אימות שיבוץ; אירועים שטרם אומתו מוצגים לבדיקה בלבד.</p>' : ''}
      <div class="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        <section aria-label="דגלים פדגוגיים"><strong>דגלים פדגוגיים:</strong> ${badges(pedagogical) || '<span class="text-slate-500">לא אותרו בנתונים הזמינים</span>'}
          <div class="text-xs text-slate-500">הדגלים, הציונים והמסננים לפי הכללים הפעילים.</div></section>
        <section aria-label="איכות נתונים"><strong>איכות נתונים:</strong> ${badges(quality) || '<span class="text-slate-500">לא אותרו בנתונים הזמינים</span>'}
          <div class="text-xs text-slate-500">שיבוצים לפי חניכים מוצגים וכל מקצועותיהם; חניכים שאינם במצבת לפי כלל קובץ הסדין.</div></section>
      </div></div>`;
  }

  function weaknessModel(roster, st, rules) {
    const prereqs = prerequisitesByGrade(roster), cells = new Map(), classes = new Map(), coreRanks = new Map();
    const failed = new Set(), riskTzs = new Set(), averages = [], teachers = new Map();
    roster.forEach(t => {
      if (!classes.has(t.classKey)) classes.set(t.classKey, { key: t.classKey, grade: t.gradeKey, total: 0, risk: 0, scores: [] });
      classes.get(t.classKey).total++;
    });
    let missedTotal = 0, uncertain = false;
    GroupAnalyzer.sadinSheet(roster, st).forEach(row => Config.withRulesForGrade(row.t.gradeKey, () => {
      const hasGroups = prereqs.get(row.t.gradeKey).hasGroups, scores = [], classScores = [];
      let fails = 0, missedForStudent = 0;
      row.subjects.forEach(s => {
        if (s.avg != null) scores.push(s.avg);
        if (excludedWeaknessSubject(s)) return;
        const rank = coreRank(s.name);
        if (rank >= 0 && (!coreRanks.has(s.name) || rank < coreRanks.get(s.name))) coreRanks.set(s.name, rank);
        const missed = confirmedMissed(s, hasGroups, row.t.tz), fail = failingSubject(s);
        uncertain = uncertain || s.missed.size > missed;
        if (fail) { failed.add(row.t.tz); fails++; }
        missedForStudent += missed;
        missedTotal += missed;
        if (!missed && (s.avg == null || (s.avg === 0 && !fail && s.missed.size))) return;
        if (s.avg != null) classScores.push(s.avg);
        const key = JSON.stringify([row.t.classKey, s.name]);
        if (!cells.has(key)) cells.set(key, { cls: row.t.classKey, subject: s.name, students: [], scores: [], teachers: new Set(), missed: 0, uncertain: false });
        const cell = cells.get(key), risk = fail || (rules.minMissed > 0 && missed >= rules.minMissed);
        cell.uncertain = cell.uncertain || !hasGroups || s.missed.size > missed;
        cell.students.push({ t: row.t, avg: s.avg, fail, missed, risk });
        if (s.avg != null) cell.scores.push(s.avg);
        cell.missed += missed;
        const names = new Set(s.entries.map(e => e.teacher).concat([...s.missed.values()].map(m => m.teacher)).filter(Boolean));
        names.forEach(name => {
          cell.teachers.add(name);
          const teacherKey = JSON.stringify([s.name, name]);
          if (!teachers.has(teacherKey)) teachers.set(teacherKey, { subject: s.name, name, scores: [], total: 0, fail: 0, missed: 0, uncertain: false });
          const item = teachers.get(teacherKey);
          const events = s.events.filter(e => e.teacher === name);
          const avg = Store.weightedAverage(events).avg;
          const subject = { ...s, avg, events, missed: new Map([...s.missed].filter(([, m]) => m.teacher === name)) };
          if (avg != null) item.scores.push(avg);
          item.total++;
          if (failingSubject(subject)) item.fail++;
          const teacherMissed = confirmedMissed(subject, hasGroups, row.t.tz);
          item.missed += teacherMissed;
          item.uncertain = item.uncertain || !hasGroups || subject.missed.size > teacherMissed;
        });
      });
      if (scores.length) averages.push(scores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / scores.length);
      const cls = classes.get(row.t.classKey);
      if (classScores.length) cls.scores.push(classScores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / classScores.length);
      if (fails >= rules.minFails || (rules.minMissed > 0 && missedForStudent >= rules.minMissed)) { cls.risk++; riskTzs.add(row.t.tz); }
    }));
    const list = [...cells.values()];
    list.forEach(c => { c.risk = c.students.filter(s => s.risk).length; c.rate = c.risk / c.students.length * 100; });
    const fullCells = list.filter(c => c.students.length >= 3);
    const ranked = (fullCells.length ? fullCells : list).sort((a, b) => b.rate - a.rate || b.risk - a.risk || natural(a.subject, b.subject) || natural(a.cls, b.cls));
    const subjectStats = new Map();
    list.forEach(c => {
      if (!subjectStats.has(c.subject)) subjectStats.set(c.subject, { full: [], small: [] });
      subjectStats.get(c.subject)[c.students.length >= 3 ? 'full' : 'small'].push(c);
    });
    const subjectRate = name => {
      const stats = subjectStats.get(name), group = stats.full.length ? stats.full : stats.small;
      return group.reduce((n, c) => n + c.risk, 0) / group.reduce((n, c) => n + c.students.length, 0);
    };
    const subjects = [...new Set(list.map(c => c.subject))].sort((a, b) => {
      const aCore = coreRanks.has(a), bCore = coreRanks.has(b);
      if (aCore !== bCore) return aCore ? -1 : 1;
      if (aCore) return coreRanks.get(a) - coreRanks.get(b) || natural(a, b);
      const aFull = subjectStats.get(a).full.length > 0, bFull = subjectStats.get(b).full.length > 0;
      return Number(bFull) - Number(aFull) || subjectRate(b) - subjectRate(a) || natural(a, b);
    });
    const grades = new Map();
    classes.forEach(c => {
      if (!grades.has(c.grade)) grades.set(c.grade, []);
      grades.get(c.grade).push(c);
    });
    const classMean = c => c.scores.length ? c.scores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / c.scores.length : Infinity;
    let riskyClasses = 0;
    grades.forEach(group => {
      group.sort((a, b) => b.risk / b.total - a.risk / a.total || b.risk - a.risk || classMean(a) - classMean(b) || natural(a.key, b.key));
      riskyClasses += group.slice(0, Math.ceil(group.length / 3)).filter(c => c.risk > 0).length;
    });
    return { cells, ranked, subjects, coreRanks, classes: [...classes.keys()].sort(natural), failed: failed.size, missed: missedTotal,
      riskyClasses, riskyStudents: riskTzs.size, riskTzs: [...riskTzs], averages, teachers: [...teachers.values()], uncertain };
  }

  const mean = values => values.length ? Util.fmt(values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / values.length, 1) : '—';
  const severity = rate => rate < 20 ? 0 : rate < 40 ? 1 : rate < 60 ? 2 : 3;

  window.SadinView = {
  getHotspotsSummary(roster, filters = this.state.sadin) {
    const model = weaknessModel(roster, filters, weaknessRiskRules(this));
    const ranked = model.ranked.filter(c => c.risk);
    return {
      model, riskStudentsCount: model.riskyStudents, riskStudentTzs: model.riskTzs,
      riskClassesCount: model.riskyClasses, totalClassesCount: model.classes.length,
      hotspotsCount: ranked.length, hotspots: ranked.slice(0, 3),
      average: model.averages.length ? model.averages.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / model.averages.length : null
    };
  },

  earlyWarningRows(roster, filters = {}) {
    return GroupAnalyzer.sadinSheet(roster, filters).map(r => normalizeRow(r, true)).filter(r => {
      const ex = Config.getException(r.t.tz);
      return !(ex.isException && ex.overrideStatus === 'twelve') &&
        Config.withRulesForGrade(r.t.gradeKey, () => [...r.subjects.values()].some(riskSubject));
    });
  },

  sadinMode(el, preserveFilter = false) {
    if (el.dataset.mode === 'full') SadinView.weaknessRiskClose.call(this);
    this.state.sadin.mode = el.dataset.mode;
    if (el.dataset.mode === 'full') this.state.sadin.view = 'sheet';
    if (!preserveFilter) this.state.sadin.strip = 'all';
    this.dirty.add('sadin'); this.showTab('sadin');
  },

  weaknessRiskToggle() {
    const st = this.state.sadin;
    if (st.weakRiskDraft) return SadinView.weaknessRiskClose.call(this, true);
    const control = document.querySelector('#sadin-mode .weak-risk-control');
    if (!control) return;
    st.weakRiskDraft = { ...weaknessRiskRules(this) };
    control.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', riskPopover());
    control.querySelector('[data-act="weaknessRiskToggle"]').setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
    updateRiskPopover(this);
    $('weak-risk-popover').focus();
  },

  weaknessRiskClose(restoreFocus = false) {
    this.state.sadin.weakRiskDraft = null;
    const pop = $('weak-risk-popover');
    if (pop) pop.remove();
    const button = document.querySelector('#sadin-mode [data-act="weaknessRiskToggle"]');
    if (button) {
      button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
      if (restoreFocus) button.focus();
    }
  },

  weaknessRiskAdjust(el) {
    const st = this.state.sadin, draft = st.weakRiskDraft, key = el.dataset.key, step = Number(el.dataset.step);
    if (!draft || !['minFails', 'minMissed'].includes(key) || ![-1, 1].includes(step) ||
        !validRiskRules({ ...draft, [key]: draft[key] + step })) {
      this.toast('הגדרת הסיכון שנבחרה אינה תקינה.', 'warn');
      return;
    }
    draft[key] += step;
    updateRiskPopover(this);
  },

  weaknessRiskReset() {
    if (!this.state.sadin.weakRiskDraft) return;
    this.state.sadin.weakRiskDraft = { minFails: 1, minMissed: 1 };
    updateRiskPopover(this);
  },

  weaknessRiskApply() {
    if (!this.requireEdit()) return;
    const draft = this.state.sadin.weakRiskDraft;
    if (!validRiskRules(draft)) {
      this.toast('הגדרת הסיכון שנבחרה אינה תקינה.', 'warn');
      return;
    }
    saveWeaknessRiskRules(this, draft);
    SadinView.weaknessRiskClose.call(this);
    this.dirty.add('sadin'); this.renderTab('sadin');
    const button = document.querySelector('#sadin-mode [data-act="weaknessRiskToggle"]');
    if (button) button.focus();
  },

  weaknessRiskChange(el) {
    if (!this.requireEdit()) return;
    const key = el.dataset.key, value = Number(el.value), current = weaknessRiskRules(this);
    if (!['minFails', 'minMissed'].includes(key) || !validRiskRules({ ...current, [key]: value })) {
      this.toast('הגדרת הסיכון שנבחרה אינה תקינה.', 'warn');
      this.dirty.add('sadin'); this.renderTab('sadin');
      return;
    }
    saveWeaknessRiskRules(this, { ...current, [key]: value });
    this.dirty.add('sadin'); this.renderTab('sadin');
    const next = document.querySelector(`[data-chg="weaknessRiskRules"][data-key="${key}"]`);
    if (next) next.focus();
  },

  selectWeakness(el) {
    const scroll = document.querySelector('#sadin-table .weak-scroll');
    const left = scroll ? scroll.scrollLeft : 0;
    this.state.sadin.cell = { cls: el.dataset.class, subject: el.dataset.subject };
    this.dirty.add('sadin'); this.renderTab('sadin');
    const next = document.querySelector('#sadin-table .weak-scroll');
    if (next) next.scrollLeft = left;
    const button = [...document.querySelectorAll('#sadin-table .weak-heat')].find(b =>
      b.dataset.class === el.dataset.class && b.dataset.subject === el.dataset.subject);
    if (button) button.focus();
  },

  openWeaknessEligibility(el) {
    this.rememberScreenFilters();
    this.resetOverviewDrillFilters();
    this.state.filters.cls = el.dataset.class;
    this.state.bagrutScopeSet = true;
    Object.assign(this.state.bagrut, { chip: 'all', drill: 'all', onlyPending: false, readinessClass: '', readinessFilter: 'all', targets: false });
    this.onFilterChange();
    this.showTab('bagrut');
  },

  openWeaknessSheet(el) {
    this.rememberScreenFilters();
    this.resetOverviewDrillFilters();
    this.state.filters.cls = el.dataset.class;
    Object.assign(this.state.sadin, { mode: 'full', view: 'sheet', strip: 'all', subject: el.dataset.subject, teacher: 'all' });
    this.onFilterChange();
    this.showTab('sadin');
  },

  openWeaknessAlerts(el) {
    const cell = this.state.sadin.cell;
    if (!cell || cell.cls !== el.dataset.class || cell.subject !== el.dataset.subject) return;
    this.alertOut = [];
    SadinView.openSadinAlerts.call(this, { cls: cell.cls, subject: cell.subject, teachers: JSON.parse(el.dataset.teachers) });
  },

  sadinSubjectChange(value) {
    const st = this.state.sadin;
    st.subject = value;
    if (st.subject !== 'all' && st.teacher !== 'all' && !teachersForSubject(this.filtered(false), st.subject).includes(st.teacher)) st.teacher = 'all';
    this.alertOut = [];
    this.dirty.add('sadin'); this.renderTab('sadin');
  },

  sadinTeacherChange(value) {
    const st = this.state.sadin;
    st.teacher = value;
    if (st.teacher !== 'all' && st.subject !== 'all' && !teachersForSubject(this.filtered(false), st.subject).includes(st.teacher)) st.subject = 'all';
    this.alertOut = [];
    this.dirty.add('sadin'); this.renderTab('sadin');
  },

  sadinSort(el) {
    const cur = this.state.sadinSort, key = el.dataset.key;
    const sameKey = (cur.key === 'class_name' ? 'homeroom' : cur.key) === key;
    this.state.sadinSort = { key, dir: sameKey && cur.dir === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc' };
    this.dirty.add('sadin');
    const box = document.querySelector('#sadin-table .sticky-scroll'), top = box ? box.scrollTop : 0, left = box ? box.scrollLeft : 0;
    this.renderTab('sadin');
    const next = document.querySelector('#sadin-table .sticky-scroll');
    if (next) { next.scrollTop = top; next.scrollLeft = left; }
    const btn = [...document.querySelectorAll('#sadin-table [data-act="sadinSort"]')].find(b => b.dataset.key === key);
    if (btn) btn.focus();
  },

  sadinSummary(sheet) {
    const graded = sheet.filter(r => r.overall != null);
    const prereqs = prerequisitesByGrade(sheet.map(r => r.t));
    return {
      avg: graded.length ? graded.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.overall, 0) / graded.length : null,
      gradedCount: graded.length,
      cleanCount: graded.filter(r => !confirmedRiskSubjects(r, prereqs.get(r.t.gradeKey).hasGroups).length).length,
      totalCount: sheet.length
    };
  },

  submittedQuestionnaireChange(el) {
    const st = this.state.sadin;
    if (el.dataset.field === 'threshold') {
      const value = Number(el.value);
      if (el.value === '' || !Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0 || value > 100) {
        el.value = st.questionnaireThreshold == null ? 60 : st.questionnaireThreshold;
        this.toast('יש לבחור רף ציון בין 0 ל־100', 'warn');
        return;
      }
      st.questionnaireThreshold = value;
    } else st.questionnaireCode = el.value;
    SadinView.renderSubmittedQuestionnaires.call(this, this.filtered(), st);
    const control = document.querySelector(`#weak-questionnaires [data-field="${el.dataset.field}"]`);
    if (control) control.focus();
  },

  renderSubmittedQuestionnaires(roster, st) {
    const box = $('weak-questionnaires');
    if (!box) return;
    const population = st.subject !== 'all' || st.teacher !== 'all'
      ? GroupAnalyzer.sadinSheet(roster, st).map(row => row.t) : roster;
    const options = new Map(), scores = new Map();
    population.forEach(t => {
      const attempts = Store.attemptsOf(t.tz), byCode = new Map();
      [...new Set(attempts.map(a => String(a.code)))].forEach(code => {
        const best = Store.bagrutBest(t.tz, [code]);
        if (!best || best.kind !== 'num' || !Number.isFinite(best.num)) return;
        byCode.set(code, best.num);
        if (!options.has(code)) {
          const catalog = Config.rulesForGrade(t.gradeKey).questionnaires || {};
          const attempt = attempts.find(a => String(a.code) === code && a.subject);
          options.set(code, (catalog[code] || {}).name || (attempt && attempt.subject) || 'שאלון ללא שם');
        }
      });
      scores.set(t.tz, byCode);
    });
    const codes = [...options.keys()].sort((a, b) => natural(options.get(a), options.get(b)) || natural(a, b));
    if (!codes.includes(st.questionnaireCode)) st.questionnaireCode = codes[0] || '';
    const threshold = st.questionnaireThreshold == null ? 60 : st.questionnaireThreshold;
    const results = population.filter(t => scores.get(t.tz).has(st.questionnaireCode) && scores.get(t.tz).get(st.questionnaireCode) < threshold)
      .sort((a, b) => natural(a.name, b.name));
    const list = results.map(t => {
      const grades = scores.get(t.tz), low = [...grades.values()].filter(n => n < threshold).length;
      return `<li><button type="button" class="weak-name" data-act="openProfile" data-tz="${E(t.tz)}">${E(t.name)}</button>
        <span>ציון ${E(grades.get(st.questionnaireCode))} · ${low} מתוך ${grades.size} שאלונים מתחת לרף ${threshold}</span></li>`;
    }).join('');
    box.innerHTML = `<h2>שאלונים שכבר הוגשו</h2><p class="weak-muted">הציון הקובע לפי הנתונים שנקלטו, גם אם דווח ציון חלקי בלבד. ציון מתחת לרף אינו בהכרח חסם לזכאות.</p>
      <div class="weak-questionnaire-controls"><label>שאלון
        <select class="inp" data-chg="submittedQuestionnaire" data-field="code" ${codes.length ? '' : 'disabled'}>
          ${codes.length ? codes.map(code => `<option value="${E(code)}" ${code === st.questionnaireCode ? 'selected' : ''}>${E(options.get(code))} · ${E(code)}</option>`).join('') : '<option>אין שאלונים עם ציון מספרי</option>'}
        </select></label>
        <label>ציונים מתחת ל־ <input class="inp" type="number" min="0" max="100" step="any" value="${threshold}" data-chg="submittedQuestionnaire" data-field="threshold"></label></div>
      <ul class="weak-students">${list}</ul>${codes.length ? (results.length ? '' : '<p class="weak-muted">אין חניכים עם ציון מתחת לרף.</p>') : '<p class="weak-muted">אין שאלונים עם ציון מספרי באוכלוסייה שנבחרה.</p>'}`;
  },

  renderSadinPanel() {
    this.renderFreshnessBar('sadin');
    const st = this.state.sadin;
    const early = st.mode !== 'full';
    const weaknessScope = document.querySelector('[data-scope-controls="weakness"]');
    if (weaknessScope) weaknessScope.classList.toggle('hidden', !early);
    const panel = $('panel-sadin');
    if (panel && !$('weakness-styles')) {
      const link = document.createElement('link');
      link.id = 'weakness-styles'; link.rel = 'stylesheet'; link.href = 'css/style.css';
      document.head.appendChild(link);
    }
    document.body.classList.toggle('sadin-weak-active', early);
    let modeBox = $('sadin-mode');
    if (!modeBox) {
      modeBox = document.createElement('div');
      modeBox.id = 'sadin-mode';
      modeBox.className = 'flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-2';
      $('sadin-table').insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin', modeBox);
    }
    const subjectSelect = $('sadinSubjectSelect'), teacherSelect = $('sadinTeacherSelect');
    const alertButton = document.querySelector('#sadinActions [data-act="sadinAlerts"]');
    if (!this._sadinControlHome && panel && subjectSelect && teacherSelect && alertButton) {
      this._sadinControlHome = { parent: subjectSelect.parentElement, actions: alertButton.parentElement, alertButton };
    }
    const home = this._sadinControlHome;
    if (home) {
      home.parent.insertBefore(subjectSelect, $('sadinFilterSelect'));
      home.parent.insertBefore(teacherSelect, $('sadinFilterSelect'));
      home.actions.insertBefore(home.alertButton, $('unknown-sadinData'));
    }
    const grade = this.state.filters.grade;
    const riskRules = early ? weaknessRiskRules(this) : null;
    const riskLabel = riskRules ? `סיכון: נכשל ${riskRules.minFails}+${riskRules.minMissed ? ` או החמצה ${riskRules.minMissed}+` : ' בלבד'}` : '';
    modeBox.className = early ? 'weak-toolbar' : 'flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-2';
    modeBox.innerHTML = early
      ? `<div class="weak-title"><h1>מוקדי חולשה</h1><p>${grade === 'all' ? 'כל השכבות' : `שכבה ${E(Util.gl(grade))}`}</p></div><div class="weak-controls">
        <div class="weak-risk-control"><button type="button" class="weak-risk-trigger" data-act="weaknessRiskToggle" aria-haspopup="dialog" aria-controls="weak-risk-popover" aria-expanded="${!!st.weakRiskDraft}">${E(riskLabel)} <span aria-hidden="true">▾</span></button>${st.weakRiskDraft ? riskPopover() : ''}</div></div>
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm weak-sheet-button" data-act="sadinMode" data-mode="full">סדין מלא</button><span class="weak-quality-slot"></span>`
      : `<button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-act="sadinMode" data-mode="early">חזרה למוקדי חולשה</button>`;
    if (early && home) {
      const controls = modeBox.querySelector('.weak-controls');
      const riskControl = controls.querySelector('.weak-risk-control');
      controls.insertBefore(subjectSelect, riskControl); controls.insertBefore(teacherSelect, riskControl); controls.appendChild(home.alertButton);
      home.alertButton.textContent = 'התראות למורים';
    } else if (home) home.alertButton.textContent = '🔔 התראות';
    let summaryBox = $('sadin-summary');
    if (!summaryBox) {
      summaryBox = document.createElement('div');
      summaryBox.id = 'sadin-summary';
      summaryBox.className = 'card px-4 py-2.5 mb-3 flex flex-wrap gap-x-6 gap-y-1 text-sm';
      summaryBox.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      summaryBox.addEventListener('click', ev => {
        const subj = ev.target.closest('[data-sadin-focus-subject]'), cls = ev.target.closest('[data-sadin-focus-class]');
        if (subj) {
          const same = this.state.sadin.subject === subj.dataset.sadinFocusSubject;
          SadinView.sadinSubjectChange.call(this, same ? 'all' : subj.dataset.sadinFocusSubject);
        } else if (cls) {
          const sel = $('fHomeroom');
          if (!sel || ![...sel.options].some(o => o.value === cls.dataset.sadinFocusClass)) return;
          sel.value = cls.dataset.sadinFocusClass;
          sel.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
        }
      });
      $('sadin-table').insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin', summaryBox);
    }
    summaryBox.innerHTML = '';
    summaryBox.classList.add('hidden');
    let questionnaires = $('weak-questionnaires');
    if (!questionnaires) {
      questionnaires = document.createElement('section');
      questionnaires.id = 'weak-questionnaires';
      questionnaires.className = 'card weak-card';
      $('sadin-table').insertAdjacentElement('afterend', questionnaires);
    }
    questionnaires.hidden = !early;
    $('sadin-table').className = early ? 'weakness-screen' : st.view === 'sheet' ? 'card overflow-hidden min-w-0' : 'tbl-wrap sticky-scroll';
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-sview]').forEach(b => {
      b.classList.toggle('hidden', early);
      b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(!early && b.dataset.sview === st.view));
    });
    const entries = Store.traineeList().flatMap(t => Store.activeCurrentOf(t.tz));
    const rawUnknown = Store.raw.grades.filter(r => !Store.trainees.has(r.tz));
    const subjects = [...new Set(entries.map(e => e.subject).concat(rawUnknown.map(r => r.subject)).filter(Boolean).map(s => GroupAnalyzer.sadinSubject(s)))].sort(natural);
    if (st.subject !== 'all' && !subjects.includes(st.subject)) st.subject = 'all';
    const teachers = st.subject === 'all'
      ? [...new Set(entries.map(e => (Store.groups.get(e.groupId) || {}).teacher || e.teacher).concat(rawUnknown.map(r => r.teacher)).filter(Boolean))].sort(natural)
      : teachersForSubject(this.filtered(false), st.subject);
    if (st.teacher !== 'all' && !teachers.includes(st.teacher)) st.teacher = 'all';
    const option = (v, label, selected) => `<option value="${E(v)}" ${v === selected ? 'selected' : ''}>${E(label)}</option>`;
    setHtml('sadinSubjectSelect', option('all', 'כל המקצועות', st.subject) + subjects.map(s => option(s, s, st.subject)).join(''));
    setHtml('sadinTeacherSelect', option('all', 'כל המורים', st.teacher) + teachers.map(t => option(t, t, st.teacher)).join(''));
    if (early) SadinView.renderSubmittedQuestionnaires.call(this, this.filtered(), st);
    if (early && st.weakRiskDraft) updateRiskPopover(this);
    if (!Store.hasData()) {
      setHtml('sadinFilterSelect', option('all', 'כל החניכים (0)', 'all'));
      return this.noRoster([], 'sadin-table');
    }
    if (!Store.loaded.grades) {
      st.strip = 'all';
      setHtml('sadinFilterSelect', option('all', 'כל החניכים (0)', 'all'));
      setHtml('sadin-table', `<div class="p-8 text-center text-slate-500">${UI.signalBadge('sadin.unknown')}<p>לא נטען קובץ ציונים שוטפים. אפשר לטעון אותו בכפתור טעינת הנתונים.</p></div>`);
      return;
    }
    const roster = this.filtered();
    if (early) {
      st.strip = 'all';
      return SadinView.renderWeakness.call(this, roster, st);
    }
    let all = early ? SadinView.earlyWarningRows(roster, st) : GroupAnalyzer.sadinSheet(roster, st);
    if (!early && st.view === 'sheet' && (st.subject !== 'all' || st.teacher !== 'all')) {
      const included = new Set(all.map(r => r.t.tz));
      GroupAnalyzer.sadinSheet(roster.filter(t => !included.has(t.tz))).forEach(r => {
        all.push({ ...r, subjects: new Map(), failing: 0, missed: 0 });
      });
    }
    if (!early) all = all.map(r => normalizeRow(r, false));
    const prereqs = prerequisitesByGrade(roster);
    const missed = r => [...r.subjects.values()].reduce((n, s) => n + confirmedMissed(s, prereqs.get(r.t.gradeKey).hasGroups, r.t.tz), 0);
    const risks = r => confirmedRiskSubjects(r, prereqs.get(r.t.gradeKey).hasGroups);
    const filters = [
      ['all', early ? 'כל החניכים לטיפול' : 'כל החניכים', all.length],
      ['fail', 'עם נכשל שוטף', all.filter(r => r.failing > 0).length],
      ['multiple', '2+ מקצועות בסיכון (שלילי/חסר)', all.filter(r => risks(r).length >= 2).length],
      ['missed', 'אירועים שהוחמצו', all.filter(r => missed(r) > 0).length]
    ];
    setHtml('sadinFilterSelect', filters.map(([key, label, count]) => option(key, `${label} (${count})`, st.strip)).join(''));
    const selectedFilter = filters.find(([key]) => key === st.strip);
    const rows = all.filter(r => st.strip === 'all' || (st.strip === 'fail' && r.failing > 0) || (st.strip === 'multiple' && risks(r).length >= 2) || (st.strip === 'missed' && missed(r) > 0));
    const summary = this.sadinSummary(rows);
    $('sadinFilterSelect').title = `${selectedFilter ? `${selectedFilter[1]} (${selectedFilter[2]}) · ` : ''}ממוצע כללי בסינון: ${Util.fmt(summary.avg, 1)}`;
    summaryBox.classList.remove('hidden');
    summaryBox.innerHTML = `<span><strong>ממוצע כללי בסינון:</strong> ${Util.fmt(summary.avg, 1)}</span>
      <span><strong>100% ציונים עוברים:</strong> ${summary.cleanCount} מתוך ${summary.gradedCount} עם ציונים</span>
      <span>מוצגים ${summary.totalCount} מתוך ${roster.length} חניכים בחיתוך</span>
      ${early ? '<p class="w-full text-xs text-slate-500">מקצועות בגרות ויחידות פנימיות לפי כללי השכבה; מסלול 12 שנות לימוד בהכרעה ידנית אינו נכלל. ממצאים לא מאומתים מוצגים לבדיקה.</p>' : ''}
      ${signalSummary(roster, rows, this.state.sim)}`;
    if (early) return SadinView.renderEarlyWarning.call(this, rows);
    if (st.view === 'flat') this.renderSadinFlat(rows);
    else this.renderSadinSheet(rows, st.subject !== 'all' || st.teacher !== 'all');
  },

  renderWeakness(roster, st) {
    const rules = weaknessRiskRules(this), model = weaknessModel(roster, st, rules);
    const selected = st.cell && model.cells.get(JSON.stringify([st.cell.cls, st.cell.subject])) || model.ranked[0];
    st.cell = selected ? { cls: selected.cls, subject: selected.subject } : null;
    const attrs = c => `data-class="${E(c.cls)}" data-subject="${E(c.subject)}"`;
    const cellButton = c => {
      if (!c) return '<span class="weak-empty">—</span>';
      const active = selected === c;
      const small = c.students.length < 3, label = small ? `${c.risk}/${c.students.length}` : `${Math.round(c.rate)}%`;
      return `<button type="button" class="weak-heat ${small ? 'weak-small-sample' : `weak-level-${severity(Math.round(c.rate))}`}${active ? ' is-selected' : ''}" data-act="sadinSelectCell" ${attrs(c)} aria-pressed="${active}" aria-label="${E(`${c.subject} · ${Config.classLabel(c.cls)}: ${c.risk} מתוך ${c.students.length} בסיכון${small ? ' · מדגם קטן' : ` · ${Math.round(c.rate)}%`}`)}">${label}</button>`;
    };
    const divider = model.subjects.findIndex(subject => !model.coreRanks.has(subject));
    const separator = index => divider > 0 && index === divider ? ' class="weak-block-divider"' : '';
    const rows = model.classes.map(cls => `<tr><th scope="row">${E(Config.classLabel(cls))}</th>${model.subjects.map((subject, index) => `<td${separator(index)}>${cellButton(model.cells.get(JSON.stringify([cls, subject])))}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('');
    const ranked = model.ranked.filter(c => c.risk).slice(0, 5);
    const subject = selected ? selected.subject : st.subject;
    const teacherRows = model.teachers.filter(t => t.subject === subject).sort((a, b) => natural(a.name, b.name)).map(t =>
      `<tr><th scope="row">${E(t.name)}</th><td>${mean(t.scores)}</td><td>${t.total ? Math.round(t.fail / t.total * 100) : 0}%</td><td>${t.uncertain && !t.missed ? '—' : t.missed}</td></tr>`).join('');
    const signals = SignalEngine.computeAll(this.state.sim).byTz;
    const unmatched = GroupAnalyzer.unmatchedIds().some(item => item.sources.includes('סדין ציונים'));
    const notEnrolled = roster.some(t => signals.get(t.tz).dataQuality.some(f => f.id === 'data.sadin_not_enrolled'));
    const quality = unmatched || notEnrolled;
    const kpi = (label, value, note) => `<section class="card weak-kpi"><h2>${label}</h2><strong>${value}</strong><p>${note}</p></section>`;
    const detail = selected ? (() => {
      const students = selected.students.filter(s => s.risk).sort((a, b) => natural(a.t.name, b.t.name));
      const list = students.slice(0, 10).map(s => `<li><button type="button" data-act="openProfile" data-tz="${E(s.t.tz)}" class="weak-name">${E(s.t.name)}</button><span>${E([s.fail ? `ממוצע ${Util.fmt(s.avg, 1)} · נכשל` : '', s.missed ? 'הוחמץ' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</span></li>`).join('');
      return `<h3>${E(selected.subject)} · ${E(Config.classLabel(selected.cls))}</h3><p class="weak-muted">מורים: ${E([...selected.teachers].sort(natural).join(', ') || 'לא ידוע')}</p>
        <div class="weak-metrics"><div><span>ממוצע</span><strong>${mean(selected.scores)}</strong></div><div><span>בסיכון</span><strong>${selected.risk} מתוך ${selected.students.length}</strong></div><div><span>הוחמצו</span><strong>${selected.uncertain && !selected.missed ? '—' : selected.missed}</strong></div></div>
        <h4>חניכים בסיכון</h4><ul class="weak-students">${list || '<li>אין חניכים בסיכון בתא</li>'}</ul>${students.length > 10 ? `<p class="weak-muted">ועוד ${students.length - 10} חניכים</p>` : ''}
        <div class="weak-detail-actions"><button type="button" class="btn btn-primary weak-primary" data-act="sadinCellSheet" ${attrs(selected)}>פתח בסדין</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost" data-act="sadinCellAlerts" ${attrs(selected)} data-teachers="${E(JSON.stringify([...selected.teachers].sort(natural)))}" ${selected.teachers.size ? '' : 'disabled title="אין מורה מזוהה בתא"'}>שלח התראה למורה</button></div>`;
    })() : '<p class="weak-muted">אין נתונים זמינים לבחירת תא בסינון הנוכחי.</p>';
    const prereqs = [...prerequisitesByGrade(roster).values()];
    const incompleteGroups = prereqs.some(p => !p.hasGroups);
    const incompleteSadin = prereqs.some(p => !p.hasSadin);
    const notices = [
      model.uncertain || incompleteGroups ? 'לא ניתן לקבוע החמצות ללא שיבוץ מאומת; הנתונים עשויים להיות חלקיים.' : '',
      incompleteSadin ? 'חסרים נתוני ציונים לחלק מהשכבות; הנתונים עשויים להיות חלקיים.' : '',
      unmatched ? 'קיימים חניכים בקובץ הציונים שאינם במצבת.' : '',
      notEnrolled ? 'קיימים ציונים ללא שיבוץ מאומת לקבוצה.' : ''
    ].filter(Boolean);
    const qualitySlot = document.querySelector('#sadin-mode .weak-quality-slot');
    if (qualitySlot) qualitySlot.innerHTML = notices.length
      ? `<button type="button" class="badge ${quality ? 'gate-warn' : 'gate-off'} weak-quality" data-act="openDataQuality" title="${E(notices.join(' '))}" aria-label="${E(notices.join(' '))}">${quality ? 'לבדיקה' : 'מידע חסר'}</button>` : '';
    setHtml('sadin-table', `<div class="weak-kpis">
      ${kpi('ממוצע שכבתי', mean(model.averages), 'ממוצע חניכים עם ציונים בסינון הנוכחי')}
      ${kpi('נכשלים בשוטף', model.failed, 'חניכים עם ממוצע נכשל, ללא החמצה בלבד')}
      ${kpi('אירוע הערכה שהוחמץ', !model.missed && (model.uncertain || incompleteGroups) ? '—' : model.missed, 'אירועים עם שיבוץ מאומת בלבד')}
      ${kpi('כיתות בסיכון', `${model.riskyClasses} מתוך ${model.classes.length}`, `שליש עליון מתוך ${model.classes.length} כיתות לפי שיעור סיכון בכל שכבה`)}</div>
      <div class="weak-layout"><div class="weak-main"><section class="card weak-card"><h2>מפת חום: כיתה × מקצוע</h2>
        <p class="weak-muted">אחוז החניכים בסיכון מתוך החניכים עם נתונים במקצוע; מדגם קטן משלושה מוצג כמספר חניכים.</p>
        <div class="weak-scroll"><table class="weak-table"><thead><tr><th scope="col">כיתה</th>${model.subjects.map((s, index) => `<th scope="col"${separator(index)}>${E(s)}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div>
        ${model.subjects.length ? '' : '<p class="weak-muted">אין נתונים זמינים בסינון הנוכחי.</p>'}
        <div class="weak-legend"><span><i class="weak-level-0"></i>נמוך עד 19%</span><span><i class="weak-level-1"></i>20%–39%</span><span><i class="weak-level-2"></i>40%–59%</span><span><i class="weak-level-3"></i>גבוה 60% ומעלה</span></div></section>
        <div class="weak-lower"><section class="card weak-card"><h2>חמשת המוקדים הבוערים</h2><div class="weak-ranking">${ranked.map((c, i) => `<button type="button" data-act="sadinSelectCell" ${attrs(c)} aria-pressed="${selected === c}"><span>${i + 1}. ${E(c.subject)} · ${E(Config.classLabel(c.cls))}</span><strong>${c.students.length < 3 ? `${c.risk}/${c.students.length} · מדגם קטן` : `${Math.round(c.rate)}%`}</strong></button>`).join('') || '<p class="weak-muted">לא נמצאו מוקדים בסיכון.</p>'}</div></section>
        <section class="card weak-card"><h2>מורים במקצוע הנבחר${subject && subject !== 'all' ? ` · ${E(subject)}` : ''}</h2><div class="weak-scroll"><table class="weak-teachers"><thead><tr><th>מורה</th><th>ממוצע</th><th>נכשלים</th><th>הוחמצו</th></tr></thead><tbody>${teacherRows || '<tr><td colspan="4">אין נתוני מורים זמינים</td></tr>'}</tbody></table></div><p class="weak-muted">התראה מוצעת רק בעבירת סף חומרה ובשיבוץ מאומת.</p></section></div></div>
        <aside class="card weak-card weak-detail"><h2>פירוט תא</h2>${detail}</aside></div>`);
  },

  /** ציון כטקסט שטוח בשורה אחת, בלי רקע או מסגרת: אדום כהה לנכשל/חסר/מסומן, אפור-כהה לעובר */
  sadinScore(value, missed = false, social = false) {
    const text = value == null ? (missed ? 'חסר' : '—') : Util.fmt(value, 0);
    const alert = missed || (value != null && !SignalEngine.isPassingScore(value, { social }));
    return `<span class="sadin-score ${alert ? 'is-bad' : value == null ? 'is-empty' : ''}">${text}</span>`;
  },

  sadinTip(subject, hasGroups = true, tz) {
    const exact = v => v == null ? '—' : String(v);
    const eventCount = new Set([...subject.events.map(e => e.key), ...subject.missed.keys()]).size;
    const eventId = e => JSON.stringify([e.groupId || '', e.eventName || '', e.date || '', e.weight]);
    const eventsById = new Map(subject.events.map(e => [eventId(e), e]));
    const lines = [`${subject.name} · ממוצע מקצוע ${exact(subject.avg)} · ${eventCount} אירועים`];
    subject.entries.forEach(e => {
      lines.push(`${e.displaySubject || e.subject} • ${e.teacher || 'ללא מורה'}${e.groupId ? ` [${e.groupId}]` : ''} · ממוצע ${exact(e.avg)}`);
      e.events.forEach(ev => {
        const item = eventsById.get(eventId({ ...ev, groupId: e.groupId }));
        const missing = item && subject.missed.get(item.key);
        const confirmed = missing && confirmedEvent(missing, hasGroups, tz);
        const value = ev.score != null ? `${exact(ev.score)}${ev.score === 0 && confirmed ? ' · הוחמץ' : ''}` :
          ev.scoreRaw || (confirmed ? 'חסר' : missing ? 'לא ניתן לקבוע ללא שיבוץ מאומת' : '—');
        lines.push(`${ev.eventName || 'אירוע'}${ev.date ? ` · ${ev.date}` : ''}: ${value} (משקל ${ev.weight})`);
      });
    });
    subject.missed.forEach(m => {
      if (!subject.events.some(e => e.key === m.key)) lines.push(`${m.eventName || 'אירוע'}${m.date ? ` · ${m.date}` : ''}: ${confirmedEvent(m, hasGroups, tz) ? 'חסר' : 'לא ניתן לקבוע ללא שיבוץ מאומת'} (משקל ${m.weight})`);
    });
    return lines.join('\n');
  },

  renderEarlyWarning(rows) {
    const prereqs = prerequisitesByGrade(rows.map(r => r.t));
    const signals = SignalEngine.computeAll(this.state.sim).byTz;
    const body = rows.map(r => {
      const p = prereqs.get(r.t.gradeKey);
      const findings = Config.withRulesForGrade(r.t.gradeKey, () => [...r.subjects.values()].filter(riskSubject).map(s => {
        const missed = confirmedMissed(s, p.hasGroups, r.t.tz), uncertain = s.missed.size - missed;
        const parts = [];
        if (failingSubject(s)) parts.push(`ממוצע שוטף ${Util.fmt(s.avg, 1)} — נכשל`);
        if (missed) parts.push(`${missed} אירועי הערכה שהוחמצו`);
        if (uncertain) parts.push(`${uncertain} אירועים לא ניתנים לאימות ללא שיבוץ מאומת`);
        return `<div><strong>${E(s.name)}:</strong> ${E(parts.join(' · '))}</div>`;
      }).join(''));
      const quality = signals.get(r.t.tz).dataQuality.some(f => f.id === 'data.sadin_not_enrolled')
        ? '<div class="text-slate-500">איכות נתונים: ציונים ללא שיבוץ — לבדיקה</div>' : '';
      return `<tr><td><button type="button" class="font-bold text-indigo-700 hover:underline" data-act="openProfile" data-tz="${E(r.t.tz)}" title="${E(r.t.name)} · ת.ז ${E(r.t.tz)}">${E(r.t.name)}</button></td>
        <td>${E(Config.classLabel(r.t.classKey))}${this.overrideBadge(r.t.tz)}</td><td>${findings}</td><td>${quality || '—'}</td></tr>`;
    }).join('');
    setHtml('sadin-table', `<table class="grid w-full text-right"><thead><tr><th>חניך</th><th>כיתה</th><th>ממצאים בשוטף</th><th>איכות נתונים</th></tr></thead>
      <tbody>${body || '<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-slate-500 py-8">אין ממצאים להצגה בנתונים הזמינים</td></tr>'}</tbody></table>`);
  },

  renderSadinSheet(rows, eventsMode) {
    const prereqs = prerequisitesByGrade(rows.map(r => r.t));
    const coreOrder = GroupAnalyzer.coreSubjects().map(s => s.name);
    const rank = s => { const i = coreOrder.indexOf(s); return i < 0 ? coreOrder.length : i; };
    /* עמודות קבועות: כל מקצועות הסדין של השכבה (ללא תלות בחיפוש, בכיתה או בסינון השורות) */
    const all = GroupAnalyzer.sadinSheet(this.filtered(false, true), this.state.sadin);
    const columnPrereqs = prerequisitesByGrade(all.map(r => r.t));
    const hasMissing = (s, r, key) => {
      const m = s.missed.get(key);
      return !!m && confirmedEvent(m, columnPrereqs.get(r.t.gradeKey).hasGroups, r.t.tz);
    };
    const names = [...new Set(all.flatMap(r => [...r.subjects.keys()]))]
      .filter(name => all.some(r => {
        const s = r.subjects.get(name);
        return s && (s.avg != null || s.events.some(hasEventData) || [...s.missed.keys()].some(key => hasMissing(s, r, key)));
      }))
      .sort((a, b) => rank(a) - rank(b) || natural(a, b));
